downward and he remained hanging forty days and forty nights until all his blood had flowed out through his nose and his mouth, and he gave up the ghost. God raised him from the dead by His power. Then, he threw himself into the middle of the river (thinking) that perhaps the big fish would eat him and (that) he would die. But the fish did not touch him at all. (He remained) immersed in the water for many days and he died. Our Lord Christ raised him again from the dead. The third time, he buried himself in the sand and died. Our Lord raised him. The fourth time, he threw himself and rolled from the top of a high mountain - there were on this mountain stones sharpened like knives -, they penetrated his sides and his whole body and he died immediately. Ezekiel, his disciple, wept over him, then our Lord raised him and comforted him. The fifth time, he threw himself from a high tree onto a large stone sharpened like a sword. He was cut into two parts and died. Our Lord raised him. The sixth time, he tied himself by his feet and remained thus forty days and forty nights and died. God sent his angel to him and (the latter) raised him from the dead and comforted him.
መልአኮ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ "ወአንሥኦ ፡ እሞት ፡ ወናዘዞ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኩነና ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ፪ጊዜ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወለለ ፡ ዙ ሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዘይመውትቶ ፡ አንሥኦ፥ ፡ እግዚእነ ። ሳብዕ ፡ ምዕረ ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ እገሪ ሁ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አፈን ፡ ዓዘቅተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ነዊኅ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ጓመዓልተ ፡ ወጓሌሊተ ፡ ወሞ ተ ። ወአስተርአዮ+፤ ፡ ፤ሎቱ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወአንሥኦ ፡ እሞት ፡ ወናዘዞ ፡ ወአጽንዖ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ የአክለከ ፡ ኦፍቁርየ ፡ ባውሊቶ፤ ፡ ፤እምዘ ፡ ትጻምው ፡ ወትቀትል4 ፡ ነፍሰከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ "ኅድገኒ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ፥ ፡ ከመ ፡ እጸሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስምከ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጸመውከ ፡ አንተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያውሩ+ ። እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወወልደ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወሞትከ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኃጥኣን ። ወንሕነሰ"-፤፤ ፡ ኢይደልወነ" ፣ ዘንተ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ትጻሙ ፡ በእንቲአነ ።
This saint tortured himself thus seven times and died seven times. Each time he died, our Lord raised him. The seventh time, he stretched his legs over the opening of a deep well, stood (thus) forty days and forty nights and died. Our Lord Christ - glory to him! - appeared to him, raised him from the dead, comforted him, strengthened him and said to him: "It is enough, my friend Bawli, you have mortified and killed yourself enough." (Bawli) said to him: "Leave me, my Lord, to mortify myself for your holy name as you mortified yourself for the race of man. Indeed, you are God and Son of God and you died because of sinners, but we, we are not worthy of this: that you should mortify yourself for us." Our Lord comforted him, strengthened him and ascended to heaven.
ወናዘዞ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወአጽንዖ ፡ ወዓርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ሰማይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አንጽና ፡ ተጋብአ፣፡'ቦቱ፣ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ አባ ፡ ባውሊ ። ወአስተርአዮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለአባ ፤ ባው ሊ፥7-5 ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ፥ ፡ 'ሥጋከ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ብሶይ ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ባውሊ፤ ፡ ረሰዩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስዱ። ፡ ሥጋሁ፣፣ ለአባ ፡ ብሶይ” ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ” ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ፣
When the holy father Abba Bsoy went to the mountain of Antinoë, the holy Abba Bawli joined him. Our Lord Christ appeared to Abba Bawli and said to him: "Your body will be with the body of Abba Bsoy." When the holy Abba Bawli died, they placed his body with the body of the holy Abba Bsoy as our Lord had told him. When they wanted to take the body of Abba Bsoy to the monastery of Scetis (= 'Asqetes) - that is to say the monastery of the holy Abba


[p. 34] {#po.44.p34}
ወነሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወኃደጉ ፡ ሥጋ ሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ባውሊ ። ወቆመት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወኢሖረት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ባውሊ" ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ "ውስተ ፡ ሐመርጾ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሖረት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወአብጽሕዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አስቄጥ ስ። ወሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ባውሊ” ፡ ይነብር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘአባ ፡ ብሶይ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡
Macarius (= Magares) -, they took the body of Abba Bsoy, they placed it in a boat and left the body of Abba Bawli. But the boat remained motionless and did not move forward. They then took the body of Abba Bawli and placed it with the body of Abba Bsoy in the boat; immediately, the boat moved forward and they brought them to the monastery of Scetis. The body of the holy Abba Bawli has remained with the body of Abba Bsoy until now in the church of Abba Bsoy. May their prayer be with us, amen.
አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ሚናስ ፡ ወሐሲና ፡ ሰማዕታት ። <ወቆጵርያኖስ ፡ ወ፻ ዕደው ፡እለ ፡ ጾሩ ፡ ሕማማተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወላህበ ፡ እሳት ፡ ውዑይ ፡ ወዮስቴና ።> ጸሎቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also the saints Minas (= Minas) and Hasina became martyrs <(as well as) Cyprian (= Qopreyänos), the three men who endured the sufferings of torture and the flame of a boiling fire and Justina (= Yostena).> May their prayer be with us, amen.

### READING FOR THE 8TH OF TEQEMT
አመ ፡ ቿለጥቅምት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መጥራ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ሰማዕታት! ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ "እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ" ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ መሃይምን ። ወሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ዳኪዮስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ አቆመ ፡ አምልኮ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ ወአመንደቦሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙቶ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ትእዛዙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፤ ወአመንደቦሙ ፡ 'ለብዙኃን ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ ወከዐወ ፡ ደሞሙ ። ወአስተዋደይዎ ፡!ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ” ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ። ወአብጽሖ ፡ መኩንን ፡ 'ለቅዱስ ፡ ኅቤሁ- ወሰአሎ፤ ፡ በእንተዝ፤፤ ፤ ወተአምነ ፡ "በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ እምአምላክ፤ ፡ ዘበአማን ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መኩንን!" ፡ ስግድ ፡ ለጣዖታት፤ ፡ ወአንሰ ፡ እሁበከ ፡ "ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ፤ ። ወኢተወክፈ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኪዳነ!" ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኪዳናቲከሰ ፡ ወንዋይ ከ ፡ የሐውሩ፤ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ። ወተምዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወይቤሎ፣7፣ አንሰ ፡ እዬንነከ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ኩነኔ! ። ወኢፈርሀ ፡ ቅዱስ፥" ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ ወኢተመይ ጠ ፡ እምክሩ ፡ ሠናይ ። "አላ ፡ ይቤሎ።፡ጮ፡ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እሰግድ ፡ ለእግዚእየ" ፡ ክርስቶስ፡ ፡ ፈጣሬ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወምድር ። እፎ ፡ ይደልወኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እኅድጎ ፡ ወእስግድ ፡ ለጣዖታ ት ፡ ዘተገብሩ፤፤ ፡ 'እምአእባን ፣ ወዕፀው፤ ፡ 'ወኢይሰምዑ ፡ ወኢይሬእዩ፡" ፡ ወኢያጹንዉ ፡ ወኢያንሶስዉ ። ወተምዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡፥ይቅሥፍ ዎ ፤ ወቀሠፍዎ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሰቀልዎ ፡ በመዝራዕቱ ፡ ወሞቅሕዎ” ፡ ወሠጠጡ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ወገበዋቲሁቶ፥ ፡ በኅለት ፡ ይቡሳት ፡ ወእግዚእነ፤ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day the holy Matra the elder became a martyr and many (became) martyrs with him. This saint was of the people of the city of Alexandria, (he was) a believing Christian. When Decius (= Däkiyos) the impious reigned, he established the worship of idols and he oppressed all the people of the Christians. His order reached the city of Alexandria; he oppressed many of its inhabitants and their blood flowed. This saint was denounced to the governor as being a Christian. The governor had the saint brought to him and questioned him about this. He confessed our Lord Christ as being a true God (from) a true God. The governor said to him: "Adore the idols and I will give you much money." (Matra) did not accept this promise from him and said to him: "Your promises and your money will go with you to hell." The governor became angry with him and said to him: "I am going to make you undergo great torture." The saint was not afraid of his torture and did not turn away from his beautiful purpose; on the contrary, he said to him: "I, I adore my Lord Christ, creator of heaven and earth. How would it be fitting for me to abandon him and adore idols which are made of stone and wood, neither hearing nor seeing, neither feeling nor walking?" The governor became angry with him and ordered him to be scourged and he was scourged with a great scourging. Then, they hung him by his arms, they imprisoned him and they broke his face and his sides with dry rods. Our Lord Christ healed his wounds and raised him without


[p. 36] {#po.44.p36}
ክርስቶስ። ፣ ይፌውስ ፣፡ ሦሰላቲሁ ፡ ወያንሥኦ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ፤ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ "በአፍአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወመተሩ?፥ ፡ ርእሶ፥ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያ ት ። ወከማሁ ፡ ፤ብዙኃን ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
damage. When (the governor) was weary of torturing him, he ordered his head to be cut off outside the city; they cut off his head with the sword and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Likewise, many became martyrs with him. May their prayer and their blessing for all of them be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት፤ ፡ ኮኑ፤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ባኮስ ፡ ወቅድስት ፡ ቢላግያ! ፡ ሰማዕት ። ወተዝካሩ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሖር ። ወተዝካራ ፡ ለሶስና ፡ ወለደቂቃ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ጸምዋሕ ፡ ወተዝካሩ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አጋቶን ፡ ባሕታዊ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለ ም ፡ አሜን።
On this day the saint Bacchus (= Bâkos) and the saint Pelagia (= Bilägyä) became martyrs. [(And there is) the commemoration of Abba Hor, the commemoration of Susanna (= Sosenä) and her children martyrs in the city of Thmoui (= Samwäh?) and the commemoration of Abba Agathon (= 'Agäton) the solitary.] May their prayer be with us forever.

### READING FOR THE 9TH OF TEQEMT
አመ ፡ ፪8ለጥቅምት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡'አብ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ሊዋርዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጸጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፤ሮሜ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ወጌር ፡ ወንጹሕ ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ ወልህቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥርዓታተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወፈጸመ” ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሕገ ፤ ወትምህርተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወኀረይዎ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት" ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ ሮሜ ። "ወሶበ ፡ ነበረ" ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጴጥሮስ" ፡ ርእሰ ፣ ሐዋርያት ፡ ወሖረ'፤ ፡ ሑረተ ፡ ሠናይተ ፤ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል ። ወኮ ነ ፡ "ወትረ ፡ ይሜህሀሮሙ፤፤ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ወይጌሥጾሙ?፤ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይትዐደዉ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father Liberius (= Liwäryos), patriarch of the city of Rome. This saint was a just, excellent and pure man. He became a monk from his childhood and grew up in the rules of the Church. || fulfilled all the law and the doctrine of the Church. They chose him and consecrated him patriarch of the city of Rome. When he was seated on the throne of the Apostle Peter, head of the apostles, he followed a beautiful path and pleased God the Most High. He constantly instructed the people, rebuked those who transgressed the commandment of God and brought them back into the path of God.
እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወይመይጦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ሞተ ፣ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ዘይንእስ ፡ "ወልደ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ፤" ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ዩልያኖስ ፡ ዓላዊ ። "ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ንጉሥ! ፡ ከሐዶ፤ ፡ "ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ! ፡ ወአመንደቦሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወተካየዶ ፡ ለቅዱ ስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አንሰ!" ፡ ሀለውኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኣመዘብር ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያና ተ ፤ ዘሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወኣጠፍኦሙ ። "ወበጸሎቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ" ፡ አጥፍኦ! ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዩልያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ ከመ፤፤ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፤ ፈነዎ። ፡ "ለቅዱስ ፡ ክቡር ፡ መርቆርዮስ ፡ ሰማዕቱ"- ፡ ወአጥፍኦ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ከሓዲ"" ፡ እስመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፣ ባስልዮስ ፡ ቆመ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ሥዕሉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ መርቆርዮስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ይቤለነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ” ፡ ያጠፍኦሙ ፡ ለአብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወእስእለከ" ፡ ከመ ፡ "ትሖር ፡ ኀቤሁ0?፤ ፡ ወትቅትሎ፡ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ወአድነነ፥ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መርቆርዮስ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሥዕ ል ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ኦሆ፤ ።
When Constantius (= Quastantinos) the Younger, son of Constantine (= Quastantinos) the Great, died, Julian (= Yulyänos) the impious reigned after him. This emperor denied our Lord Christ and oppressed the people of the Christians. He made a promise to the saint Basil (= Bäselyos) and said to him: "I, I am going to devastate the churches of the people of the Christians and destroy them." Thanks to the prayer of the saint Basil, God destroyed Julian the impious. This (took place) thus: God sent the saint (and) illustrious Mercurius (= Marqoryos) his martyr, and he destroyed the impious one; indeed, the saint Basil stood before the icon of the saint Mercurius and said to him: "This impious one told us that he would destroy the churches. I ask you to go to him and kill him in the battle." The saint Mercurius in (his) icon bowed as if he were saying yes


[p. 38] {#po.44.p38}
ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ዩልያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ፥ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እኅተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ። ወአርኃወ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ ወአመንደቦሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ወኩነኖሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡፤ እምኔሆሙ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ሊዋርዮስ ፣ መጽአ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቂሳርያ ፡ ወተጋብአ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ወተሰነአዉ ፡ ክልኤሆ ሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወይትጋብኡ ፡ በዩልያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ወይሚጥዎ ፡ እምክ[ህ]ደቱ ፡ ወእምስሕተቱቶጾ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ያአምርዎ ፡ እምንእሶሙ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይትመሀሩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ኅቡረ ። ወተንሥኡ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ወመጽኡ“ፈ፤ ፡ ኅበ ፡ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወበጽሑቶ0፤ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዩልያኖስ። ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቆሙ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወይፈቅዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትናገርዎ ፡ በዘ ፡ ይመይጥዎ ፡ እምስሕተቱ ። ይቤሎሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኀበ፥ ፡ አይቴ ፡ ኀደግምዎ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ ፀራቢ" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሳለ ቅ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እግዚእነ" ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኀደግናሁ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ለከ ፡ ሣጹነ ፡ "ሶበ ፡ ትመውት ፡ ይቅብሩከ፥" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ። ተምዓዓ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሞቅሕዎሙ ፡ ወሞቅሕዎ፤ ፡ "ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ። ፡ ሊዋርዮስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ። "ወርእየ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በንዋም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ሙቁሕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መርቆርዮስ፥ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ በአማን ፡ ኢየኀድጎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ይጽርፍ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አምላኪየ ። ወነቅሀ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱ ስ" ፡ እምንዋሙ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስጾ ፡ ዘርእየ ። ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡
This Julian the impious was the son of a sister of the father of Constantius (= Constance). He opened the temples of the idols, oppressed the people of the Christians and (had) many of them tortured. This saint Liberius came from the city of Rome to the city of Caesarea (= Qisäryä) and met with the saint Basil. They both agreed to go to the city of Antioch (= 'Ansokiyä), to meet with Julian the impious and turn him away from his impiety and his error, for they had known him since their childhood when they studied with him together. They both rose, came to the city of Antioch and arrived before the emperor Julian. When they stood before him and wanted to speak with him to turn him away from his error, he said to them: "Where have you left the carpenter's son?" mocking our Lord Christ. The saint Basil answered him and said to him: "We have left him so that he might make you a coffin so that, when you die, they will bury you in hell." (Julian) became angry with them and ordered them to be imprisoned, and they imprisoned this saint Liberius with the saint Basil. This saint (= Liberius) saw in (his) sleep, while he was imprisoned, that the saint Mercurius was saying to him: "In truth, I will not let this impious one blaspheme my God." This saint awoke from his sleep and told the saint Basil what he had seen. The saint Basil had also seen this vision.
ካዕበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ራእየ"" ። ወእምድኅረዝ፤ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ዜና ፡ ቅትለቱ ፡ ለዮልያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ወነግሠ“ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ዮማንዮስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ የአምን ፡ በእግዚእነ" ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወአውፅኦሙ” ፡ "እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ወሐሩ ፡ ለለ 8እምኔሆሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሚመቶሙ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ መርቆርዮስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘቀተሎ ፡ ለዩልያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ በስእለቱ ፡ ለቅዱ ስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ። ወሖረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሊዋርዮስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወተቃረኖሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ 'ወአውገዞሙ ፡ ወሰደዶሙ ፡ እምሚመቱ”" ። ወሖረ ፡ ሑረተ ፡ ሠናይተ ፤ ወአሥመሮ ፡ "ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ?፤ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ እምድኅረ” ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ "በሚመቱ ፡ ሄዓመተ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡
After that arrived the news of the death of Julian the impious and Jovian (= Yomänyos) reigned after him - he believed in our Lord Christ. He brought the saints out of prison and each of them went to his charge. As for the saint Mercurius, it is he who killed Julian the impious at the request of the saint Basil. This saint Liberius went to the city of Rome, fought much against the Arians, excommunicated them and drove them from the (territory of) his charge. He followed a beautiful path, pleased our Lord Christ and died in peace after having spent six years in his charge. May God have mercy on us by his prayer and by his intercession, amen.
ወበትንብልናሁ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፣ አትናቴዎስ”" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘአንጾኪያ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መነኮሰ ፡ ባሕታዌ ፣ ወጽሙደ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላኩ ፡ የዋህ ፡ ወትሑት ፡ ወፍጹም ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ምግበረ ፡ ሠናይ ። ወተጋብኡ ፡ ኩሎ ሙ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆልሳት ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ። ወለአኩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እልምፍርያን'" ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ስልቅ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል” ፡ ናሁ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሊ ቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ነዓ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ በጐጐዓ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሚም ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡
[On this day also died Abba Athanasius (= 'Atnätéwos), patriarch of Antioch (= 'Ansokiyä). As he was a monk, an anchorite, full of devotion towards the Lord his God, gentle and humble, accomplished in every beautiful manner of acting, all the bishops gathered to him to name him patriarch. They dispatched to the Maphrian (= 'Elmefreyän) bishop of the city of Seleucia (= Selq), saying: "Behold, our father, the patriarch Abba Michael (= Mikä'el), is dead; come quickly in haste so that we may name a


[p. 40] {#po.44.p40}
እስመ ፡ ኃረይነ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ጸድቀ ፡ ዘይደሉ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ሚመት ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ እስመ ፡ ንጸንሐ ከ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይሠይም ፡ ዘእንበሌከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ አበዊነ ።
patriarch, because we have chosen a just man who is worthy of this pure charge (and) because we are all waiting for you and (that) no one except you (can) consecrate him according to the ordinance of our fathers."
ወሶበ ፡ ጐንደየ ፡ ምጽአተ ፡ እልምፍርያን ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ጸኒሖሙ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ዛዕለት ፡ ጫምዎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ለመናብርቲሆሙ ። ወለእልምፍርያንኒ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ለአኩ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወበጽሐ”" ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዓምድ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ሶርያ ፣ ወተራከበ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ዘአንጾኪያ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ጐንደየ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ሜ ሙ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ እልምፍርያን ፡ ተምዓ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ከመዝኑ ፡ ሠዓርክሙ ፡፥ ሕገ ፤ ዘማእከሌነ ፡ ወማእከሌክሙ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ አውገዘ ፡ ኩሎ፣ ዘይዜከር ፡ ስሞ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አትናቴዎስ” ፡ እመሂ ፡ በዮርባን ፡ አው ፡ በዕጣን ። ወዘን ተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ሀገሮ ።
As the coming of the Maphrian the bishop was delayed, having waited for fifty days, (the bishops) consecrated Abba Athanasius and went away to their (respective) seats. As for the Maphrian the bishop, he came according to what the bishops had dispatched to him, arrived at the city of Amida (= 'Amed) at the border of Syria (= Soryä) and met with a bishop of Antioch. (The latter) told him that, as his (= Maphrian's) coming had been delayed, they had consecrated the patriarch. Upon hearing (this), the Maphrian became greatly angry and said: "Is this how you violate the law which (is) between us and you?" Having said this, he excommunicated anyone who would mention the name of Abba Athanasius either during the mass or during the incensing. Having said this, he returned to his city.
ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ተከዘ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለረድኡ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ተአዘዝ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወግበር ፡ ዘእቤለከ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትባርክ ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ አንሰ ፡ አሐውር ፡ ርሑቀ ፡ መጠነ ፤ አሐቲ ፡ ዓመት ። ወለእመጾ5። ፡ ኃሠሠኒ ፡ ሕዝብየ ፡ በሎ ሙ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጾማዕቱ ፡ ወአንተ ፡ አዝዞሙ ፡ በቃልየ ፡ እስር ፡ ወፍታሕ ፡ ወይቤ ሎ፡፡ ረድኡ ፡ ኦሆ ። ወለብሰ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ነዳያን ፡ ወሖረ ፡ በእግሩ ፡ እስ ከ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሰልቅ ። ወጐድጐደ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዐቃቤ ፡ አንቀጽ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ትፈቅድ ፡ ወይቤ፤ ፡ በረከተ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ወነጊሮ ፡ ዓቃቤ ፡ አንቀጽ ፡ አብ ኦ ፤ ለ]አባ ፤ አትናስዮስ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ እምአይቴ ፡ መጻእከ5 ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ሶርያ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ ኃጢአየ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወዓራዘ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙት ፡ በታሕተ ፡ ጽላሎተ ፡ ጸሎትከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ቀሲስኑ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወሚመ፣ ዲያቆን ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አልቦ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ ለመጋቢ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያንብሮ ፡ ምስ ለ ፡ መነኮሳት ።
When Athanasius learned of this remark, he was very saddened and said to his disciple: "My son, obey me and do what I tell you to bless my soul; for me, I am going far away for a year. If my people look for me, tell them: 'He is in his cell'. You, command them by my voice, bind and loose." His disciple said to him: "Yes". Abba Athanasius put on the clothing of the poor and went on foot until he arrived at the city of Seleucia. He knocked at the door of the bishop and the gatekeeper said to him: "What do you want?" (Athanasius) said: "The blessing of my father the bishop." Having told (this to the bishop), the gatekeeper introduced Abba Athanasius. The bishop said to him: "From where have you come?" (Athanasius) said to him: "I have come from the land of Syria lacking daily food and the clothing of the year to die under the shadow of your prayer." The bishop said to him: "Are you a priest or a deacon?" (Athanasius) said: "No". Then (the bishop) called the steward and ordered him to install him (= Athanasius) with the monks.
ወአባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይትጌበር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ቤት ፡ እስከ ፡ ቀዲሐ ፡ማይ፡ ወኩስትሮ ፡ ኩስሐ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ ወበቅል ፡ ወአንደዶ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወጸቢሖ ፡ ፀብሕ ፡ ወሐቢ ጾ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ወኢተርፎ ፡ ምንትኒ ፡ እምተግባረ ፡ አኃው ። ወሶበ ፡ ይፌጽም ፡ ግብረ፤ ቤተ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወይቀድኅ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወያነድድ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ወይኩስትር ፡ አብያተ ፡ እስከ ፡ አፍቀርዎ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወመሰሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ሰማያዊ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አስተብቀዕዎ ፡ ለጳጳስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚም ፡ ዲያቆነ ። ወበዕለተ ፡ እድ ፡ ቦ አ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ወጸውዖ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚሞ ፡ ዲያቆነ ፡ በከየ ፡ መሪረ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኅድገኒ ፡ አባ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ነዳይ ፡ ኢይደሉ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ መዓርግ ። ወሶበ ፡ አኃዞ ፡ በጽንዕ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አባ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ብዝኃ ፡ ኃጣውእየ ፡ ኃደጉ ፡ መልእክተ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ አንከረ ፡ እምትእግሥቱ ።
Abba Athanasius did all the domestic tasks even to drawing water, cleaning the excrement of the horses and mules, lighting the fire, preparing the sauce and baking the bread. || nothing was missing for him of (what constitutes) the work of the brothers. When he had finished the work of the bishop's house, he went into the houses of the monks, drew water for them, lit the fire and swept the houses so much so that they loved him much and (that) it seemed to them that he was a heavenly angel. Then they begged the bishop to ordain him deacon. On Sunday, (the bishop) entered the church and called Abba Athanasius to ordain him deacon. (The latter) wept bitterly and said: "Leave me, Father, I, a poor (man), am not worthy of this rank." When (the bishop) seized him by force, (Athanasius) said to him: "Forgive me, Father, for I am a deacon, but, because of my many sins, I have abandoned the (divine) service." Upon hearing (this), the bishop was astonished at his constancy.


[p. 42] {#po.44.p42}
ወነበረ ፡ ፪አውራኃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትለአክ ፡ በግብረ ፣ ዲሞና ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ አእምሮቶ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወፈቀደ" ፣ ይሚሞ ፡ በግብረ ፡ ቀሲስ ። ወበእለተ ፡ እድ ፡ ቦ አ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወጸውዖ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱ ስ ፡ጸውዓከ ፡ዮም ፡ እሠይመከ ፡ ቀሲሰ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ታሕተ፣፤ እግረ ፡ አብ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አንብዕ ፡ ወአስተብሞዖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅድጎ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየኃድጎ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ መሐረኒ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ቀሲስ ። ወሰሚዖ ሙ ፡ አብ ፡ ወመነኮሳት ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወአክበርዎ ፡ ፈድፋደ ።
(Athanasius) remained seven months serving in the diaconal office. When the bishop saw his great knowledge, he wanted to place him in the priestly office. On Sunday, the bishop entered the church, called him and said to him: "The Holy Spirit has called you today, I am going to ordain you priest." Upon hearing (this), Abba Athanasius fell at the feet of the father with tears and begged him to leave him. When he understood that he would not leave him, he said to him: "Have mercy on me, my father, for I am a priest." Upon hearing (this), the father and the monks rejoiced in him and honored him much.
ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ አብ ፡ ጣዕመ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ወውሳኬ ፡ መልእክቱ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚሞ ፡ ጳጳሰ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘሞተ ፡ ጳጳሰ ፡ ዚአሃ ። ወጸውዖሙ ፡ ለጳጳሳት ፡ ወለኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ለቀሳውስትሂ ፡ ወለዲያቆናትሂ ፡ ወለመነኮሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚም ዎ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ እጉድ ። ወጸውዕዎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ 'ጸውዓከ ፡ ዮም፤፡ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ መዓርገ ፡ ጵጵስና ፡ ዘመንበረ ፡ እገሌ ። ወአስተብቀ-'ዖሙ” ፡ ወአምሐሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይሚምዎ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየኃድግዎ ፡ በከየ ፡ መሪረ ፤ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘአንጾኪያ ፡ ወምሥጢረ ፡ ምጽአቱሂ ፡ ዜነዎሙ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ አውፅአ” ፡ አክሊሎ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ምውት ፡ ሰዓተ ፡ ነዋኃ ። ወተንሚ ኦ ፣ ጸርሐ ፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ ወለሕዝብ ፡ ኦአኃውየ ፡ ንግሩኒ ፡ እስኩ ፡ ዘእግበር ፡ አንሰ ፡ እፈርህ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይረድ ፡ እሳት ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወኢያውፅየኒ ፡ ወምድርኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢታብቁ ፡ አፉሃ ፡ ወኢተኃጠኒ ፡ መፍትውኑ”” ፡ ይትለአክ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘአንጾኪያ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ዓመተ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይቀድሕ ፤ ማየ ፡ ወይኩስትር ፡ 'ቤተ ፡ ወኩስሐ ፡ አፍራስ ፣ ወበቅል” ፡ ከመ ፡ ገብር ።
Then, when the father saw the sweetness of his speech and the importance of his service, he wanted to name him bishop in a city whose bishop had died. He summoned the metropolitans, the bishops, the priests, the deacons and the monks to consecrate him on Sunday. They called Abba Athanasius and said to him: "Today, God has called you to the episcopal rank of such and such a seat." He begged them and adjured them not to consecrate him. When he understood that they would not leave him, he wept bitterly and told them that he was Athanasius, the patriarch of Antioch, and he also narrated to them the mystery of his coming. Upon hearing this speech, the father took off his crown, fell to the ground and was as if dead for long moments. Having risen, he cried out and said to the bishops and the people: "My brothers, tell me, I pray you, what I must do, (for) I, I fear now that fire will fall from heaven and consume me and that the earth will open its mouth and swallow me up. Was it necessary that Athanasius, the patriarch of Antioch, served a year drawing water and cleaning the house and the excrement of the horses and mules like a slave?" Upon hearing (this), the bishops and the people prostrated themselves before him (= Athanasius) and kissed his hands and his feet.
ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወሕዝብ ፡ ሰገዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወሰዓምዎ፤"" ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ያምጽኡ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ክህነት ፡ ምኩሐ ፡ ወመስቀለ ፡ ወበትረ ፤ ክህነት ፡ ወያንብርዎ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መንበር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጾርዎ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መታክፍቲሆሙ ፡ምስለ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ወአብእዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአዖድዎ ፡ ፻ጊዜ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ አክስዮስ ፡ አክስዮስ ፡ አክስዮስ ፣ ዝብሂል ፡ ይደልዎ ፡ ይደል ዎ ፡ ይደልዎ ። ወእምዝ ፡ 'አልባሰ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ለብሰ፤!፤ ። ወቀደሰ ፡ ወአቅረቦሙ ፡ ወባረ ከ ፡ ሀገሮሙ ፡ ሚመጠነ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ፋርስ ።
Then, the bishop ordered magnificent priestly garments, a cross and a priestly staff to be brought, and for him (= Athanasius) to be placed on a seat. Then, the bishops carried him on their shoulders with his seat and introduced him into the church. They made him go around it three times saying: "Aksyos, aksyos, aksyos!", which means: "He is worthy, he is worthy, he is worthy!". Then, (Athanasius) put on the garments of consecration, consecrated (the host), administered (communion) to them and blessed their city. How great the joy that there was for the inhabitants of Fars (= Persia)!
ወአመ ፡ ሳኒታ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ አብ ፡ በቅለ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዘይትፈቀድ፤። ፡ ለፍኖት ፡ ወአስተፀዓኖ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ውስተ፤" ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳ ት ፡ ወኤሏኢስ ፡ ቆጳሳት ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ በእገሪሆሙ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ለእልምፍርያን ፡ ተፀዓን ፡ አንተ ፡ ወንሖር ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሐሰ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ በከመ ፡ መጻእከ ፡ አንተ፤" ፡ በእግርከ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሀገርየ ፡ አነሂ ፡ ኣበጽሐከ ፡ እስ ከ ፤ ሀገርከ፤ ፡ በእግርየ ። ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ከመ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ተቀበልዎ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወኤሏስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ ወለእልምፍርያን ኒ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሰልቅ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መንበር ፡ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ዐቢየ ፡
The following day, the father brought a mule and everything that was necessary for the journey: he had Abba Athanasius mount it (to go) to the city of Antioch with the metropolitans and the bishops walking on foot. Abba Athanasius said to the Maphrian: "Mount in the saddle, you, and let us go together." (The Maphrian) said to him: "Far from me (that), my lord! Just as you, you came on foot to my city, I too, I will take you back to your city on foot." The metropolitans of Antioch having learned that the patriarch Abba Athanasius had arrived, the metropolitans (therefore) and the bishops welcomed him as well as the Maphrian, the bishop of the city of Seleucia. They placed him (= Athanasius) again on the (patriarchal) seat and rejoiced with a great joy.


[p. 44] {#po.44.p44}
ፍሥሐ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አስተፋነውዎ፤ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ ወተመይ ጡ ፡ ሀገሮሙ ፡ በሰላም ። ሐይወ!፤" ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ "ዝንቱ ፡ አብ፤ ፡ ወረዓየ ፡ መርዔቶ ፡ በጽድቅ ፡ ወበርትዕ ፡ ወአዕረፈ፤ ፣ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
Then, the patriarch (= Maphrian) and the bishops took leave of him (= Athanasius) and returned to their city in peace. This father lived a beautiful life, pastured his flock with justice and righteousness and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus in the ages of ages.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ቀዳሜ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘአንጾኪያ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኒቆምዮን ፡ እሁ ፡ ለፋሲለደስ ። እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ዘመድ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወአቡሁኒ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምዓበይተ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወባዕል ፡ ጥቀ ፡ በወርቅ ፡ ወበብሩር ፡ ወበአልባሳት ፡ ክቡራት ፡ ወአልቦሙ ፡ ጐልቀ፣ ለአግብርቲሁ ፡ ወለአዕማቲሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጥቀ፤፤ ፡ ያፈቅሮ ፡ "ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤፤፤ ፡ ወይመጸውት ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡ ወፍቁር'፤" ፡ በኀበ ፡ ኩሉ ፤ ሰብእ ። ወሶበ ፤ ወለድዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሐፀንዎ ፡ በተግሣጽ ፡ 'ዐቢይ ፡ ወሠና ይ!፤፤ ፡ ወመሐርዎ ፡ መዝሙረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወመጻሕፍተ ፡ ብሊት ፡ ወሐዲስ ፡ እስከ ፡ መልአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወተምህረ ፡ ተፅዕኖ ፡ ፈረስ ፡ ወደርብ ዮ ፡ ኩያንው ፡ ወነዲፈ ፡ ቀስት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ወገላውዴዎስ ፡ በቤተ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፋሲለደስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እጉሁ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ተሠምየ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ፋሲለደስ ። ወአዝማዲሁኒ ፡ ኩሎሙ!፤፤ ፡ ፈራህያነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወየሐውሩ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ኩነኔ ሁ!፤፤ ፣ ወአልቦ ፡ እምውስቴቶሙ ፡ ዘያቄርር ፡ ልቦ ፡ እምላህበ ፡ ፍቅሩ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፣ ወዘይትገኃሥ ፡ እምፍናዊሁ ፡ ኢለየማን ፡ ወኢለፀጋም ።
[On this day also died the saint Stephen (= 'Estifänos), protomartyr of Antioch (= 'Ansokiyä), the son of Niqomyon brother of Basilides (= Fäsiladas). Indeed, this blessed Stephen was of an illustrious family and his father was of the great men of Antioch. (He was) very rich in gold, in silver and in precious garments, innumerable were his servants and his handmaidens; he loved our Lord Jesus Christ much, gave alms to the poor and the needy and was loved by all the people. When (his parents) had begotten this saint, they raised him in a great and beautiful discipline and they taught him the psalms of David and the books of the Old and New (Testaments) so much so that he was filled with the knowledge of the Holy Spirit. He learned to ride a horse, to throw throwing weapons and to shoot with the bow with Victor (= Figtor) and Claudius (= Galawdëwos) in the house of the saint Basilides, for he was the son of his brother; because of that, he was called son of Basilides. His parents also all feared God and followed all his judgment. There was none among them whose heart was cold far from the flame of the love of Christ nor who turned away from his ways to the right or to the left.
ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጽንዓ ፡ አፍቅሮቶሙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ያውርሶሙ ፡ መንግሥቶ ፡ ወጽድቆ ፡ ዘአስተዳለወ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጠር ፡ ዓለም ። ወእምዝ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ኖላዌ ፡፣ አጣሊ ፡ እምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዕደው ፡ ዘአኃዝዎሙ ፡ በምክንያተ ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ወስሙ ፡ አግሪጳዳ፤፤ ፡ ወእምንእሱ ፡ ኃደ ረ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ቦቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሖረ፤! ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ረሰይዎ ፡ ዐቃቤ ፡ አፍራስ። ወሶበ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ 'ተዓርቀ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እምነ ፡ መንበር!! ። ወአሐተ" ዕለተ ፡እንዘ ፡ ታንሶሱ ፡ ወለተ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ በተስላሰ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ርእየቶ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያዘፍን ፤ አፍራሲሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ የአነዝር ፡ ሎሙ ፡ እንዚራ ። አሜሃ ፡ ፈተወቶ ፡ ወተወስበት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ረሰየቶ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ወሠመየቶ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ። አይ ፤ ዓመፃ ፡ ወአይ ፡ ርስዓን ፡ ዘተገብረ ፣ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ክህዶ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወአምለከ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ ወቀተለ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘየአምን ፡ በክርስቶስ ። ኮነ ፡ በላዔ ፡ ሥጋ፤!፤ ፣ ወሰታዬ ፡ ደም ፡ ከመ ፡ አራዊት ፡ መሠጥ ፡ ወዘረዎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ኅሩያነ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ከመ ፡ አባግፅ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ኖላዊ ። ቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘቀተለ ፡፣ በኩናት ፣ ወበቅንዋት ፡ ወቦ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘሰደዶሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ በሐውርተ ፡ ግብጽ ፡እንዘ ፡እሱራ ን ፡ በሐዊዩን ፡ ውስተ፤" ፡ አፉሆሙ ፡ ልጓም ፡ እስከ ፡ መልአ ፡ ብካይ ፡ ወሰቆቃው ፡
When God saw the strength of their love for him, he wanted to make them inherit his kingdom and his justice that he had prepared for them before the world was created. Then Satan brought a man, a shepherd from the land of Egypt (from the number) of the men that had been taken because of the war. His name (was) Agrippada; from his childhood, the spirit of Satan dwelt in him. When he went to Antioch, they made him prefect of the horses. When the king of Antioch died, a king was missing from the throne. One day, while the king's daughter was walking on the terrace of her house, she saw him (= Agrippada) making his horses jump at the same time that he was playing a musical instrument for them. She then desired him and married him so much so that she made him king, and she called him Diocletian (= Diyogletyänos). What injustice and what impiety were committed in his time after he had denied Christ! He adored the idols and killed anyone who believed in Christ. He was a flesh-eater and a blood-drinker like wild predatory beasts. He scattered all the elite (men) of the (previous) kings like sheep without a shepherd; there were among them whom he killed with a spear and arrows; there were among them whom he drove into the regions of Egypt,


[p. 46] {#po.44.p46}
ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ወብእሲት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምታ ፡ አብ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ እጐ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እሁ ፡ ወዓርክ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዓርኩ ፡ እስመ ፡ መልአ ፡ ደመ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ፣ ምድር ፤ ወሥጋሆሙኒ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ግዱፈ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽጐ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይቀብሮሙ ፤ እንበለ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ውስተ፤፤፤ ፡ ውሂቦሙ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ለእለ ፡ የዓቅቡ ።
chained with iron, a bit in the mouth, so much so that tears and lamentations filled the whole land of Antioch. The man (wept) for his wife, the woman for her husband, the father for his son, the son for his father, the brother for his brother, the friend for his friend, for the blood of the saints filled all the earth, their bodies were thrown on the square of the city and no one buried them except by giving money in secret to those who guarded (the bodies).

This saint Stephen dwelt praying to God day and night so that he would give him the crown of martyrdom. At the beginning of his impiety, Diocletian wrote a letter (distributed) throughout his territory saying: "(I,) Diocletian, the powerful king, I order in the territory of my kingdom to close the churches, to open the temples of the idols and to adore my gods, they who gave me victory in the battle. If there is someone who does not adore them and who does not prostrate himself before them, his house will be pillaged and he will be punished by various punishments. No one will spare the rich nor the poor, the great nor the small."

The king then gathered all the troops and all the great men of his kingdom and he ordered that this letter be read before (those who were) gathered. When the saint Stephen heard (read) (this) impure letter, his whole being burned with the fire of a (holy) zeal: he considered Diocletian as absolutely nothing, cursed him and was shaken in his soul. He said: "What is this new injustice that I hear said that you show in this city, king, by this doctrine of yours and by writing this impure (and) blasphemous letter of yours which (is) of Apollo (= 'Apelon) whose faithful my Lord Jesus Christ annihilates?" Then, the saint Stephen showed himself courageous, gathered his strength, leaped upon the soldier in whose hand (was) his (= Diocletian's) impure letter and snatched it (from him). The king and all the great men of the people saw him and he tore (the letter) into pieces. The king said to him: "Stephen, what have you done? You wanted to bring ruin upon yourself."

Then the king drew his sword which (was) in his hand and sliced him (= Stephen) through the middle and he was in two parts. The head of the saint Stephen remained a long moment before the king. It rebuked him and told him what would happen to him at his end, how he would receive alms at the gate of the city of Antioch, (how) his two eyes would be blinded and (how), after that, he would be destroyed. (The head) cried out much and mentioned all the saints of the city of Antioch who would become martyrs. The story of the head of Stephen was heard in all the cities (of the kingdom) of Antioch so much so that a whole numerous crowd gathered to see the head rebuking the king and telling him his ruin before the multitude.

nailed to the ground, she spoke yet more. This head remained nailed to the ground for three days, and all heard it speaking and rebuking the king, like the head of Yohannes (= John) the Baptist when he rebuked Hërodes (= Herod) in those days. Indeed, Hërodes and Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) were of the same nature. Hërodes was seduced by fornication and by drunkenness (due to) wine, so much so that he killed a prophet such as women had not borne one like him. By killing this just man, he found shame, and as for his wife, the earth swallowed her; his daughter also became mad. As for him, his body produced worms and rotted, and he died in misery while trying to hide his fornication. The head of Yohannes rebuked him publicly. As for Diyogletyänos, while he wanted to publish the greatness of the idols and their worship, the head of the saint 'Estifänos (= Stephen) rebuked him and published against him that his gods (were) demons. It prophesied that his kingdom would be destroyed, (that) his eyes would be blinded, (that) he would receive alms at the city gate, and (that) he would die in misery.
ወሶበ ፡ ነጸረ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ቅዱስ፤የ ፡ ኢታረምም ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ "ንፍቅ ፡ ታቦት ፡ ዘዓረር፤፤፤ ፡ ወይሕትሙ ፡ አፉሃ ፡ ወይግድፍዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ሌሊተ ። ወበከመዝ ፡ ግብር ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ስምዖ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወረደ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወአውዕፅኣ ፡ ለንፍቀ ፡ ዓረር ፡ ወአንበራ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ባሕር ። ወእንዘ ፡ ተኃሥሥ ፡ እሙ ፡ በድኖ ፡ በጽሐት ፤ በፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀለወት፤ ፡ ንፍቀ ፡ ዓረር ፡ ወነሥአቶ ፡ እምህየ ፡ ወአንበረቶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤታ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኃለፈ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ስደት ፡ ወአኅተወት ፡ ቀንዲለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብሪሁ ፡ ዘየሐቱ ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኃለፈ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ስደት ፡ ሐነጹ፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ሠናይተ ፡ ወተገብረ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ጐልቀ፥ ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡
When Diyogletyänos saw that the head of the saint would not be silent, he ordered it to be put in a leaden chest, for its opening to be sealed, and for it to be thrown into the sea by night. It was in this manner that the saint 'Estifanos completed his martyrdom. Then the angel of God descended from heaven, took out the leaden chest, and placed it on the seashore. While his mother was searching for his corpse, she arrived by the will of God where the leaden chest was. She took it from there, placed it in her house until the days of persecution had passed, and lit a lamp in his tomb that burned day and night. After the days of persecution had passed, a beautiful church was built for him, and miracles and wonders without number occurred there. May his holy blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.
ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ፤ ፡ አመ ፡ 8፻ ወ ፳አመተ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ንጹሓን ፡ ወቅዱሳ ን ፡ በመንግሥቱ ፡ ለኢዮብ ፡ ጌር ፡ ወበመዋዕለ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ መንክር ፡ ወመድምም ፡ ዘያደነግጽ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ወለእለ ፡ ሰምዕዎ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ፅለተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ጸልመ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ ጽልመተ ፡ እስመ፤ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መዓል ት ፡ ከመ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወአስተርአዩ ፡ ከዋክብት ። ወአብርሁ ፡ ማኅትወ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወፈር ሁ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ፍርሃተ ፡ ወአውየዉ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ልቦሙ ፣ ወሣህሉ ፡ ምሕረቶ፤፥ ፡ ዘአሰፈዎሙ ፡ ወአብዝኃ ፡ ምሕረቶ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ። ወአሰሰለ ፡ ፍርሃተ ፤ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወተከሥተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማድ ፣ ወአብርሃ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዓለመ ፡ ወጠፍኡ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መሐትው ። ወኮነ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ጽልመተ ፡ ፀሐ ይ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፳ሰዓት ፡ እመንፈቀ ፡ ቿሰዓት ፡ እስከ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ 8ሰዓት ፣፡ ወተወ ሰከ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ዘኢያፍጠነ ፣ ሙስናሆሙ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ኃጢአቶሙ ፡፣
[On this day also, in the nine hundred and fifth year of the pure and holy martyrs, under the reign of 'Iob (= Job) the excellent, and in the time of the patriarchate of abbä Qêrellos (= Cyril), there was in the universe a prodigious and astonishing thing that terrified those who saw it and those who heard it that day: the sun darkened with complete darkness; indeed, the day was like the night. The stars appeared and the people lit lamps. They felt great fear, cried out to God, praised him with all their heart, and implored his mercy which he had promised them. (Then) he increased his mercy for them, he removed fear from them, the light was revealed, and the sun came out as usual. It illuminated the whole universe and all the lamps were extinguished. The duration of the darkening of the sun in it (= in the universe?) was one hour, from the middle of the eighth hour until the middle of the ninth hour. Glory was increased for God who did not hasten their (= men's) ruin because of their sin - on the contrary, he (showed) quickly his mercy and his pity


[p. 50] {#po.44.p50}
ወባሕቱ ፡፤ አፍጠነ ፡ ሣሀሎ ፡ ወምሕረቶ፤፤ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወለእግዚእነ ፡ 'ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ ሎቱ፤ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ፤ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ። በረከቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፤ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
for them, - and for our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him for ever and ever, amen!] [On this day also (is) the commemoration of abbä Sem'on (= Simon) the bishop. May his blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu for ever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ገብረ ፣ ቶማስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰብክ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ህንደኬ ፡ አመ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አብኔስ ፡ ሐያጢ!' ። ወሖረ ፡ አብኔስ ፡ ከመ ፡ የአምፕ ፡ ለጐንዶፎር ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወተናገሮ ፡ በእንተ ፡ፀራቢ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ዘአምጽኦ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወተፈሥሐ'፤፤ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ምንት ፡ "ግብርከ ፣ ወኬንከ፤" ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ አነ ፡ ፀራቢ ፡ ወነዳዊ ፡ ወእገብር ፡ ዕርፈ ፡ ወአርዑ ተ ፡ ወመዳልወ ፡ ወመንኩራኩረ ፡ ወአሕማረ ፤ ወመቃድፈ ፡ ወታዕካ ፡ ነገሥት ። ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘየሐንጽ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ማእዜ ፡ ተሐንጽ ። ወይቤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፪አውራኅ ፡ አመ ፡ ሠርቀ ፡ ኅዳር ፡ ወእፌጽም ፡ በወርኃ ፡ ሚያዝያ ። ወአንከረ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሕንፃ ፡ 'ይትሐነጽ ፤ በሐጋይ" ፡ ወዝሰ! ፡ "በክረምት ፡ የሐንጽ! ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ "ብዙኃ ፡ እምወርቅሂ ፡ ወእምብሩርሂ"፤ ፡ ለርእሱ ፡ ወለእለ ፡ ይትቀነዩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወተመጢዎ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ምጽወተ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል!፤" ፡ እሁብ ፡ ዘንጉሥ ፡ ለንጉሥ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወዓሊሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጠይቅ ፡ ለእ መ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ታእካ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ታእካሰ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ወጠፈሩ ፡ ተርፈ ። ወዳግመ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወወሀቦ፤ ፡ ብሩረ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ፈጽም ፡ ፍጡነ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ መጽአ፤ ፡ ወሐተተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ታዕካ ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ ታእካ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዳእሙ፤ ፡ ዘመጽዋተ'፤!፤ ፡ ንዋየከ ፡ ለነዳያን ። ወተምዕዓ ፤ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሞቅሖሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያ ፡ ወለሠያጢ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይመክር ፡ በዘይቀትሎሙ ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡፤ ሐመ ፡ ጋዶን ፡ እሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወሞተ ፣ ወአርአይዎ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ታዕካ ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ሐዋርያ፤ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ጐንዶፎር ፡ እሁ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ዘመኑዝ ፡ ታዕካ፤ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ዘእቱከ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ሐዋርያ፤” ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ። ወሶበ ፡ ገብአት ፡ ነፍሱ ፣ ለጋዶን ፡ ነገሮ ፡ ለእኃሁ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተባደሩ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ፣ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወአውፅእዎሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያ ፡ "እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ፤ ፡ ወለሠያጢ ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ወተጠምቁ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እድ ፡ ወአንስ ት ፡፤ ወተ[መ]ጠዉ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወደሞ ፡ ክቡረ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አንበረ፤”" ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወባረኮሙ ፡ ወወሀቦሙ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ እምኀቤሆሙ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከ ቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also Tomäs (= Thomas) the apostle performed a miracle while preaching in the land of Hendakë (= India) when he entered it with 'Abnès (= Abbanes) the deceiver. 'Abnès went to greet King Guendofor (= Gundafor). He spoke to him about the carpenter Tomäs whom he had brought with him. The king rejoiced and said to him (= to Tomäs): "What is your occupation and your trade?" The apostle said: "I am a carpenter and architect, and I make plows, yokes, scales, wheels, ships, oars, and palaces." The king rejoiced, led him to a place (where) he was building, and said to him: "When will you have (finished) building?" The apostle said: "In two months; (I will begin) at the new moon of hedär and I will finish in the month of miyäzya." The king was astonished and said: "All construction is built in summer, and this one (= Tomäs) builds in winter!" Then, he gave him much gold and silver, for himself and for those who would work with him. Having returned, the apostle gave it as alms to the poor and the needy, saying: "I give what (belongs) to a king to a king." Then, the king sent his messenger to the apostle to recognize if the palace was finished. The apostle said to him: "The palace is finished, but its roof remains (to be finished)." The king dispatched a second time, gave him much money, and said to him: "Finish quickly." Then, when (the king) came and inquired about the palace that Tomäs had built, he was told: "He has not built any palace except that of the alms (given) from your wealth to the poor." The king became angry and imprisoned the apostle and the merchant until he had deliberated on how he would kill them. That day, Gädon (= Gad) the king's brother fell ill and died. The angels showed him the palace that the apostle Tomäs had built for King Guendofor his brother. He said to them: "To whom does this palace belong?" [15 They said to him: "To your brother the king (belongs this palace) which the apostle Tomäs, who is in prison, built for him." When the soul of Gädon returned, he told this to his brother. Then, all ran together and went to the prison. They brought the apostle out of the prison as well as the merchant, believed in Christ, and were baptized, men and women, in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, and they received his holy body and his precious blood. Then (Tomäs) laid (his hands) upon them, blessed them, gave them salvation, and went away from them. May his prayer and his blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]

[On this day also (is) the death of Däwit (= David), king of Ethiopia, who is called "the winnowing fan of the wicked" and "the wall of diamond". May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.]

### READING OF THE 10 TEQEMT

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father, virgin (and) pure 'Awmänyos (= Eumenes), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; he is the seventh since the apostle Mark. The saint 'Abrimyos (= Primus) ordained this father deacon, and he remained in the diaconate ten years. When Yestos (= Justus) was named patriarch and (he) saw the virtues of this saint, his right faith, and his knowledge, he ordained him priest. He ordered him to teach the believers the rule and the doctrine of the holy Church in the church of Alexandria. When the father Yestos died, this father was named patriarch, and he handed over the affairs of the churches and the instruction of the believers to the father Markeyänos (= Marcianus). || constantly disputed with the impious and the pagans, turned sinners from their error, and revealed to them in an evident manner the divinity of our Lord Christ. He told them that he was equal with his Father and the Holy Spirit: one single divinity. || remained in his office thirteen years and died on the tenth of the month of teqemt in the sixth year of the reign of the emperor 'Antonyos (= Antoninus Pius). In his time the saint Sofyà (= Sophia) and her three daughters were martyrs. May God have mercy on us through their prayer to all, amen.
ሰማዕታት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀረነ ፡፤ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ፤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሰርጊዮስ" ፡ ሰማዕት፤፤ ፡ ቢጹ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ባኮስ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ” ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሰርጋቲያ ፡ ዘእምጽርሐ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ መክስምያኖስ ፣ "እምቤተ ፡ መንግሥት?" ። ወሶበ ፡ ኩነኖሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ፈነዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡፤ አንጥያኮ ስ፤ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ሶርያ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ኩነኖ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ባኮስ ፡ ወቀሠፎ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ እስከ ፡ መጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውግሩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ አፍራጥስ ። ወሰወሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሐይቅ። ወፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አንባሰ ፡ ወተኩላ ፡ ወዓቀብዎ ፡ እስከ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ እለ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ በህየ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ፣ ባኮስ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ በኀቤሆሙ ። ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሰርጊዮስ"" ፡ የሐዝን ፡ ወይበኪጾ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ወርእየ ፡ ራእየ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ከመ ፡ እሁ ፡ ባኮስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽርሕ ፡
On this day also the saint Sargiyos (= Sergius), companion of the saint Bäkos (= Bacchus), became a martyr. This saint was of the sargätiyä who (were part of) the imperial palace of Maksemyänos (= Maximian); (he was) of the royal house. When the emperor punished them (= Bacchus and Sergius), he sent them to 'Antyäkos (= Antiochus) in the land of Soryä (= Syria). This one (had) the saint Bäkos tortured and (had) him scourged with a great scourging until he gave up his soul. He ordered his body to be thrown into the river of 'Afrätes (= Euphrates). God protected him and the river brought him to the bank. God sent him a lion and a wolf, and they guarded him until God gave an order to some saints who were dwelling there; (these) went, took the body of the saint Bäkos, and deposited it at their place. The saint Sargiyos was afflicted and wept for him. He saw a vision at night according to which his brother Bäkos was in a

beautiful palace (which) shone greatly. His soul was greatly consoled because of what he had seen. After that, the governor ordered long iron nails to be driven into the feet of the saint Sargiyos and he sent him to the city of Rusäfä with horsemen. || ran with them when they ran and his blood flowed on the ground. They met on the way a young virgin girl and they drank water (given) by her. She felt pity for him and had mercy on his youth and his beautiful appearance. He said to her: "Come, follow me to the city of Rusäfä to take my body." This virgin followed him to the city of Rusäfä. The governor gave an order in these words: "If Sargiyos does not obey and (if) he does not sacrifice to our gods, let his head be cut off." And his holy head was cut off with the sword by the authority of the governor. This governor did not want to cut off the head of the saint Sargiyos, for he was the friend of the saint and had received this position thanks to him. The young virgin girl approached the saint Sargiyos, (she had) with her wool hairs and she collected there the precious blood that had flowed from the neck of the saint.
ወምስሌሃ ፡ "ፀጉረ ፡ ፀምር" ፡ ወነሥአት ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ደሞ ፡ ክቡረ ፤ ዘውሕዘ ፡ እምክሳዱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ። ወዓቀቡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ” ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጸሜተ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ስደት፤ ፡ ወሐነጹ ፡ ሎቱ” ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ሠናይተ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ሩጻፋ ፡ ወቀደስዋ ፡ ፲ወ፳ ኤኢስ ፡፤ ቆኦሳት ። ወአንበሩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ታኣምራት ፤ ወመንክራት ፡ ዓበይት ። ወይእቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ሀለወት ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፤ ወሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ሰርጊዮስ፥- ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ እንዘ ፤ ይውሕዝ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ቅብዓ ፤ ዕፍረት ፤ ዘጹናሁ ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግምዔ" ፡ ዘመርመር ፡ ፈውስ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ዱያን ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡፤ ወትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡
They kept the body of the saint until the end of the days of persecution; they built for him a beautiful church in the city of Rusäfä and fifteen bishops consecrated it. They placed his holy body there and great miracles and wonders occurred from it. This church still exists today. The body of the saint Sargiyos (is) there, a perfumed oil with a very sweet odor flowing from it into a marble basin, a remedy for all the sick. May his prayer, his blessing, and his intercession be with us for ever and ever, amen.
ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ዕረፍቱ ፣ ለዮሐንስ ፡ መስተጋድል ። ወእምብዝኃ ፡ ጽድ ቁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይትላጸይ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ኢያርሕሶ ፡ በማይ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለአባ፣ ገብረ ፡ ናዝራዊ ፤ ዘቃውት ፡ ወመለያልያ ፡ ሰማዕት ። በረከቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፤ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also (is) the death of Yohannes (= John) the (spiritual) fighter. Because of his great justice, when he shaved his head, he did not wet it with water; indeed, he was the father of abba Gabra Näzräwi of Qäwt … May his blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]

### READING OF THE 11 TEQEMT
አመ ፡ ፲፳ለጥቅምት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አፅረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ክቡር! ፡ አባ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጸጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the illustrious father abbä Yä'qob (= Jacob), patriarch of the


[p. 56] {#po.44.p56}
አንጾኪያ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በጽሖ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወሰደድዎ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ሃይማኖ ት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ስደት ፡ 'ዓመታተ ፡ ብዙኃ" ። 'ወእምዝ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወፈነዉ ፡ ወአብጽሕዎ ፡ ወነበረ" ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሰደድዎ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ አርዮስ ። ወነበረ ፣ "ውስተ ፡፤ ዳግማይ ፤ ስደት፤" ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀረነ ፡ ለኩልነ ፡ በጸሎ ቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወበጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ አሥመርዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር በምግባሮሙ ፡ ሠናይት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈት፤፤ ፡ ቢላግያ'፤ ። ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት?" ፡ ጸድቅት ፡ ኮነ ት ፡ እምሀ[ገ]ረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ ከሓዲያነ ። ወኮነት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ አጥረየት ፡ ምስለ፤ ፡ ርኩስ፤ ፡ ሃይማኖታ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ሑረታ! ፡ ካዕበ!" ። ወውእቱኬ?፤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ትነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ተውኔት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትዋነይ ፤ ወትረ" ፡ ወትሳለቅ ፡ ወትዘፍን ፡ ወትዘሙ" ። ወረከባ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘስሙ፣ ጳውሎስ ፡ ወገሠጻ ፡ ወአዘከራ ፡ በእሳተ ፡ ገሃነም ፡ ወኩነኔ ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልባ ፡ ተግሣጹ ፡ ወአምነት ፡ በእግዚእነ።፤ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወተአምነት ፡ ሎቱ፡፡ ፣ በኩሉ ፡ ዘገብረት ። ወአጽንዓ ፡ ልባ ፡ ወመሀራ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትምትር ፡ ተስፋሃ ፡ እምንስሐ" ፡ ወኢእምሕረተ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፤ አጥመቃ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡፣ ክርስትና ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፤ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ" ፡ ወበርሃት ፡ በጸጋ ፣ ጥምቀት" ። ወእምዝ ፡ ቦአት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ንስሓ ፡ በልብ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ወሕሊና ፡ ንጽሕት” ፡ ወአጻመወት ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ወአኅሠረቶ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ለብሰት ፡ ልብሰ ፡ እደው ፡ ወሖረት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወሰገደት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ "እለ ፡ በሀገረ ፤ ኢየሩሳሌም፤ ። ወተጋብአት" ፡ በኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ወፈነዋ ፡ ውእቱ?" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፳8እምነ ፡ አድባራተ ፡ ደናግል ፡ ዘአፍአ ፡ ሀገ ረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወነበረት ፡ ውስቴቱ፥ ፡ ወለብሰት ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወተፀምደ ት ፡ ወተጋደለት ፡ መጠነ ፡ ዓዓመት ፡ ወአሥመረቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈት ፡
city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch). A great misfortune happened to this saint: he was exiled because of the right faith and he remained in exile for many years. Then, the people of the city of 'Ansokiyä gathered, sent for him, and made him come (back). He remained (at 'Ansokiyä) a few days. After that, the Arians exiled him again, he remained in (this) second exile seven years and died in peace. May God have mercy on us all through the prayer of this saint and through the prayer of all those who have pleased God by their beautiful way of acting, for ever and ever, amen. On this day also died Bilägyä (= Pelagia). This just saint was from the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch) and her parents were infidels. She also had, along with her impure faith, an impure conduct. This (was) thus: she lived in a house of pleasure, playing incessantly, laughing, dancing, and fornicating. A holy man, a bishop whose name (is) Päwlos (= Paul), found her, admonished her, and reminded her of the fire of hell and the eternal punishment. His admonitions entered into her (= Bilägyä's) heart, she believed in our Lord Christ through his intermediary, and she confessed to him (= to Päwlos) everything she had done. || strengthened her heart and instructed her so that she would not cut off her hope from the penance and the mercy of God. Then he baptized her with Christian baptism in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, and she shone with the grace of baptism. Then she entered into penance with a firm heart and a pure spirit; she tormented her body and mortified it. Then she put on a man's garment, went to Jerusalem, and prostrated herself in all the churches that (are) in the city of Jerusalem. She went to abbä 'Eleskenderos (= Alexander), patriarch of the city of Jerusalem, and he sent her to one of the convents of virgins that (are) outside the city of Jerusalem. She remained there, put on the monastic garment, and practiced asceticism and (spiritual) combat for forty years. She pleased God and died in peace. May her prayer be with us for ever and ever, amen.
በሰላም ። ጸሎታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተጋድሎተ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ አርማሜ ። በረከቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also (is) the (spiritual) combat of the martyr 'Armämé. May the blessing of this just man be with us for ever and ever.]

### READING OF THE 12 TEQEMT
አመ ፡ ፲፪ለጥቅምት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። ፤ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ 'ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት" ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. [On this day (is) the commemoration of the glorious angel Mikä'ël

እስመ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ፣ "ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እሴይ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለዳዊት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ልሔም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅብዖ ፡ ለዳዊት ፡'ወልዱ ፡ ይንግሥ ፡ላዕ ለ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ለሳኦል ፡ ወልደ ፡ ቂስ ። ወሖረ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፅሴይ" ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አቅርብ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ውሉደከ ፡ ወአምጽኦሙ ፡ ሎቱ፤ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዳዊት፤፤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ!" ፡ የዓቅብ ፡ አባግዓ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገራህቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ አልዓ ለ ፡ ሳሙኤል" ፡ ቀርነ ፡ ቅብፅ ፡ ዘነገሥት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡ ለውሎዶ ፡ ዕሜ ይ! ፡ ኢሠምረ! ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ለዕሜይ! ፡ ኢተርፈ ፡ መኑ ሂ ፡ እምውሉድከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ 'ተርፈ ፡ ወሬዛ!" ፡ ዘይንእስ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ የዓቅብ ፡ አባግዓ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገራህት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ አምጽኦ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወአምጽኦ፣ ሎቱ ። 'ወሶቤሃ ፡ ነሥአ፤ ፡ ቀርነ ፡ ቅብዕ ፡ ዘነገሥት ፡ ወቀብኦ ፡ ወሠምረ" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት፤፤ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወረድኦ" ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ እስከ ፡ ቀተሎ ፡ ለጎልያድ ፡ ረዓይታዊ ፣ ኢሎፍላዊ ፤ ወአድኃኖሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አዘዙ ፡ መምህራነ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወሠርዑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ለሚካኤል ፡ አመ ፡፲ ወ፪ለለወርጉ።
(= Michael), the archangel: on this day God sent him to Sämu'ël (= Samuel) the prophet while he was in the Temple, and he ordered him to go to the house of 'Esëy (= Jesse), the father of Däwit (= David), in Bétalehëm (= Bethlehem) to anoint Däwit his son (so that) he would reign over the children of Israel in place of Sä'ol (= Saul) son of Qis (= Kish). Sämu'ël went to 'Esëy and said to him: "Make your sons approach for me." ('Esëy) brought them to him except Däwit, because the latter was guarding the sheep in his field. When Sämu'ël raised the horn of the oil of the kings over the heads of the sons of 'Esëy, (he saw that) God did not accept him. Sämu'el said to 'Esëy: "Does not someone of your sons remain?" ('Esëy) said to him: "There remains the adolescent who is younger than them. He is guarding the sheep in the field." Sämu'ël said to him: "Bring him to me." And he brought him to him. Then (Sämu'el) took the horn of the oil of the kings and anointed him; God accepted him and he reigned over the children of Israel. On this day God sent Mikä'ël the archangel to Däwit the prophet, he helped him and gave him strength so that he killed Golyäd (= Goliath), the gigantic Philistine, and delivered the children of Israel. Because of that, the doctors of the Church have prescribed and instituted to make the commemoration of the feast of Mikä'ël on the twelfth of each month. May his intercession be with his scribe for ever and ever.]
ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጸሓፊሁ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ወሰማዕት ፡ ዘእም ፲ ወ ፪ሐዋርያት ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሰበከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወሜጦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ከመ" ፡ ይባእ ፡ ውስ ተ፤ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ረከቦ ፡ አሐዱ፡” ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አንተሰ ፡ ኢትክል ፡ ትባእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ትላጺ ፡ ሥዕርተ ፡ ርእስከ ፡ ወጽሕመከ ፡ ወትነሥእ!፥ቦ፣ በእዴከ ፡ ሆሳዕና ፡ ወገብረ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሬዛ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይተክዝ፤ ፡ በእንተዝ፥" ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ 'በአምሳል ፤ ዘያአምሮቶ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ውእቱ፤ ፡ ወሬዛ ፣ ዘተናገሮ ፡ ቀዳሚ?" ፡ ወአጽንዖ ፡ ወናዘዞ ፡ ወተሰወረ ፡ እምኔሁ ።
On this day is the holy apostle Mätëwos (= Matthew), evangelist and martyr, who (is in the number) of the twelve apostles. After he had preached in the cities of the priests, he converted them to the knowledge of God. This (took place) thus: when he wanted to enter the city of the priests, a young man met him and said to him: "You cannot enter this city without shaving the hairs of your head and your beard and (without) taking in your hand a (palm) branch." (Mätëwos) did as the young man had prescribed (to him). While he was afflicted because of that, our Lord Christ appeared to him then in the aspect in which he knew him - (that of) the young man who had spoken to him before; he strengthened him and comforted him and disappeared from his (sight),
ወሶበ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ከመ፣ ውእቱ›፤ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምነ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምኩራበ ፡ አጵሎን ። ወረከቦ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ካህናት ፡፤ ወተናገረ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አማልክት ። ወአጠየቆ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኢኮኑ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ትእምርተ፤ ፡ ወሠረቀ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወወረደት ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ማዕድ ፡ እምሰማይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ አርሚስ ፡ ካህን ፡ ዘንተ። ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ መኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ስሙ፣
When the saint Mätéwos entered this city, he appeared to them (= to the inhabitants) as being one of the priests and he went to the temple of 'Apelon (= Apollo). The chief of the priests met him and spoke with him about the gods. The saint Mätëwos taught him that they were not gods. He performed a miracle before him: a light shone upon them and a table descended from heaven near them. When the priest 'Armis (= Hermes) saw that, he said to Mätëwos: "What is the name of your God?" (Mätëwos) said to him: "The name of my God is


[p. 60] {#po.44.p60}
ለአምላክከ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለአምላኪየ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አምነ ፡ አርሚስ ፡ ካህን ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ሕዝብቶ፥ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውዕይዎሙ ፡ በእሳት ። ወበውእቱ ፡ጊዜ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ "ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአንሥኦ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እሞት" ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አምነ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ "ወአምኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፣ ወሜመ" ፣ ሎሙ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሰ፤፤፣ ወካህናተ ፡ ወሐነጸ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ" ።
Christ." Then 'Armis the priest believed in our Lord Christ and many people (believed) with him. When the king of the city learned of this affair, he ordered them to be burned in the fire. At that moment, the king's son died. The saint Mätëwos begged our Lord Christ and (the latter) raised the king's son from the dead. Then the king believed and all the people of the city believed. (Mätëwos) appointed for them a bishop and priests and built a church for them.
ወወፅአ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አህጉር ፡ እለ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ወሜጦሙ፥ ፡ ወአብኦሙ፥ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእም[ቅ]ድመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡፣ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ብፁዓዊያን ፡ ወርእዮ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ 'ይበጽሕ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ፥ ፡ 'ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ሕፃናት- ፡ ፲1፳ወ፪፳ወጣ፻ "ወኩሉ ፡ ለለ ፡ በዓል፤ ፡ ይበጽሕ“" ፡ ኅቤሆሙ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ። ወእሙንቱ ሰ ፡ ብፁዓዊያን ፡ እለ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዞሲማስ ፡ ገዳማዊ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ስምዓ ፤ በገድሎሙ ፤ ወግብሮሙ ፡ ዘመንክር ።
He went out to the cities that (are) outside, converted them, and made them enter into the knowledge of God. Before that, he had gone to the city of the Blessed and had seen our Lord coming to them all the time; with him (there were) the souls of one hundred and forty-four thousand children. At each feast our Lord Christ comes to them and his disciples with him. These Blessed (are) those to whom the hermit priest Zosimäs (= Zosimus) went and he was a witness of their (spiritual) combat and their prodigious actions.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወምድረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወጸሐፈ ፡ ወንጌሎ ፡ በልሳነ ፡ ዕብራይስጥ ።
After that, the saint Mätëwos, the evangelist apostle, came to the city of Jerusalem and to the land of Yehudä (= Judah) and he wrote his gospel in the Hebrew language.

Then he went out to the cities that (are) outside and met in a city a man who was imprisoned - indeed, he was visiting the prisoners; he owed his master money that (the latter) had given him to trade with and (to) derive a profit, but he had lost (this money) at sea. The saint Mätëwos the apostle said to this man: "Go to such a place and, what you will find there, take (it) and give it to your master." The man went and found a purse that (was) full of gold, he took (it), gave it to his master, and was freed. The story of the saint Mätëwos became known in the city and many people believed in our Lord Christ. When Festos (= Festus), the governor of this city, learned (this), he became very angry and ordered the head of the saint Mätëwos the apostle to be cut off and his body to be thrown to the birds. They cut off his holy head with the sword. Believing people came, took his holy body, and placed (it) in a holy place. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

On this day also died the holy father, virgin, pure, struggling against desire (and) conqueror of his temperament, abbä Demêtreyos (= Demetrius), patriarch of the city of Alexandria. In the number of the patriarchs he is the twelfth. This saint was a plowman of the people who did not know how to write and worked in

a vineyard. His parents had married him to a woman from his childhood and he remained with her forty-eight years, until he was named patriarch. They (= the spouses) (were) pure in their virginity. No one knew (this) about them except God who knows what (is) in the souls and the hearts.
ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበጾ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዩልያኖስ” ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘእምቅድሜሁ ፤ አስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ "ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእ ቱ፤ ፥ ይከውን ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡እምድኅሬሁ ።ወወሀቦ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ፤!‹፤ ፡ በሳኒታ ፡ ይመጽአ፤"" ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ አስከለ ፡ ወይን ። አኀዞ ፡ ወጸሊ ፡ ላዕሌሁ፤፥ ፡ ወሚሞ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ።ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሀ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዩልያኖስ፤" ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ ለኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ወለካህናት ፡ እለ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ምስሌሁ፤ ፡ በዘርእየ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ድሜጥር ዮስ፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በጽባሕ!"" ፡ ረከበ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ድሜጥርዮስ፤ ፡ ውስተ፣ ዓጸደ ፡ ወይኑ ፡ አሐደ ፡ አስከለ ፡ ወይን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ "ጊዜ ፣ ፍሬሁ፤' ፡ ለወይን ፡ ወነሥኦ፤፤ ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ለአባ፤፤፤ ፡ ዩልያኖስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትባረክ ፡ እምኔ ሁ ። ወአኀዞ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዩልያኖስ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ወለካህና ት ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳቲክሙ ፡፤ ዘእምድኅሬየ'፤ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየ'!፤ ፡ ወአኀዝዎ ፡ ወአሠርዎ ፡ እስከ፤! ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ዩልያኖስ ። ወፈጸሙ ፡ ላዕሌሁ! ፡
When the death of the saint Yulyänos (= Julian), the patriarch who (was) before him, approached, an angel of God appeared to the latter and indicated to him about this saint that he would be patriarch after him. [He] gave him a sign about him and said to him: "Tomorrow, a man will come to you, (he will have) with him a cluster of grapes. Seize him, pray over him, and name him patriarch." When the saint Yulyänos awoke, he told the bishops and the priests who were dwelling with him what he had seen and how the angel of God had spoken to him about the saint Demêtreyos. When it was morning, this saint Demêtreyos found in his vineyard a cluster of grapes before the time of the flowering of the vine. He took it and brought it to abbä Yulyänos the patriarch so that it would be blessed by him. The saint Yulyänos seized him by the hand and said to the bishops and the priests: "This is your patriarch who (will be) after me." Then he prayed. They seized him (= Demêtreyos) and bound him until abbä Yulyänos died. They completed the prayer over him, they consecrated him patriarch, and he was filled with heavenly grace.

He learned many sciences, memorized all the books of the Church and their interpretation. He spoke on many sciences and it is he who fixed the calculation of the epact, for the people of the Christians celebrated the feast of the baptism and, from the day after it, fasted forty days; at the time of the week of the Passion, he celebrated the feast of the Passion of our Lord separately. || had acted thus since the days of the apostles until the days of this father. This father dealt with the epact by the Holy Spirit, fixed the calculation of the holy fast, and sent of his own accord a letter (on this subject) to each of the patriarchs of the city of Rome, of the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch), of the city of 'Éféson (= Ephesus), and of the city of Jerusalem. (These) rejoiced at it and established it until this day.
ኢየሩሳሌም፤፤ ። ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወሠርዕዎ ፡ እስከ፤ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወኮነ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ድሜጥርዮስ" ፡ በእንተ ፡፣ ንጽ[ሕ]ናሁ ። ወኮነ፤፤ ፡ ይኔጽሮ ፡ ለእግዚእነ፤። ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በጊዜ ፥፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ርባ ን ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይመጥዎሙ፤፤ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይደልዎሙ ፡ ወይሰድዶሙ ፡ ለኃጥኣን! ። ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ድሜጥርዮስ፤ ፣ ይከሥት ፤ ለሕዝ ቡ፤፤9 ፣ ኃጢአቶሙ ፡ እለ፤?" ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ በሕቡእ ፡ ወይገሥጾሙ!፤ጾ ፡ ለ፳8እምኔሆሙ ፡
God was with this father Demêtreyos because of his purity. || saw our Lord Christ at the moment of the consecration of the host while administering with his hand the holy mysteries to those who were worthy of them and while driving away the sinners. The saint Demêtreyos revealed to his people the sins that they had committed in secret and he rebuked each (man) of this (people) for the
