Abbä Bämoy did nothing hastily. He questioned our Lord Christ about abbä Yohannes so that he might reveal his will to him. An angel of God appeared to him, saying to him: "God tells you to accept this brother, for he will become a chosen tool." When abbä Bämoy heard this word from the angel of God, he brought abbä Yohannes into the church, shaved the hair of his head, placed (on the altar) the monastic garments, and stood in prayer for three days and three nights. After that, an angel of God appeared to him, made the sign of the cross on the monastic garments, and clothed abbä Yohannes in them. Abbä Yohannes began (to practice) great austerities, numerous (ascetic) struggles (and) beautiful deeds. One day, abbä Bämoy wanted to test abbä Yohannes. He drove him away from him, saying: "I cannot dwell with you." Abbä Yohannes stood seven days outside at the door of the monastery. Each day, abbä Bämoy would go out to abbä Yohannes and strike him with a stick and abbä Yohannes would prostrate himself before him and say: "Forgive me, my father." On the seventh day, abbä Bämoy the old man went out to go to the church and saw seven angels and, with them, seven crowns, and they were placing them on the head of abbä Yohannes. Immediately, abbä Bämoy took abbä Yohannes and brought him into his cell. From that day, (the latter) was honored and respected.


[p. 112] {#po.44.p112}
ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ አባ ፡ ባሞይ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወአብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓቱ ። ወእምይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወግሩም ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ረከበ ፡ አባ ፡ ባሞይ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ይቡሰ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ንሣእ ፡ ዛተ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ወትክላ ፡ ወአስተያ"?" ፡ እስከ ፣ ትሠርፅ ፡ ወትፈሪ ። ወተአዘዘ ፡ ቅዱስ" ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ባሞይ ፡ ወነሥኣ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡፤ ፅፅ ፡ ወተከላ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ያሰትያ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ፪ጊዜ ። ወኮነ ፡ "ማይ ፡ ርሑቅ፣ እምኔሆሙ4+፥፤ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ምዕራፍ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፻ዓመት ፡ ሠረጸት ፡ ይእቲ ፡
One day, abbä Bämoy found a (piece of) dry wood, he gave it to abbä Yohannes and said to him: "Take this (piece of) wood, plant it and water it until it sprouts and bears fruit." The saint abbä Yohannes obeyed his (spiritual) father, abbä Bämoy; he took the (piece of) wood and planted it, and he watered it twice every day. The water was distant from them (= the monks) by


[p. 114] {#po.44.p114}
ፅፅ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወፈረየት ። ወነሥአ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ 'አባ ፡ ባሞይ፣0፣ እምፍሬሃ ፡ ወወሰደ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ አረጋዊያን ፡ መነኮሳት ፡፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ንሥኡ ፡ ብል ዑ ፡ እምፍሬ ፡ ተአዛዚ ፡ ወትሕትና ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ዘንተ ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘይሁብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ጸጋ" ፡ ለአለ ፡ ይትኤዘዙ ፤ ለመምህራኒሆሙ ።
twelve stades. Three years later, the (piece of) wood sprouted, became a large tree, and bore fruit. The old man abbä Bämoy took of its fruit, brought (some) to all the old monks and said to them: "Take, eat of the fruit of obedience and humility." When the monks saw this miracle, they marveled greatly and praised God who gives this grace to those who obey their masters.
"ወኮነ ፡ አባ ፡ ባሞይ ፡ ደወየ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደዌ ፣ መጠነ ፡ ፲ወቿዓመት ። ወአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ፤ ፡ ይትለአኮ። ፡ ወኢይብሎ ፡ አሐተ ፡፤ ፅለተ ፡ሠናየ ፡ ገበርከ ። "እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ አባ ፡ ባሞይ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አረጋ ዌ ፡ ዘያመክር፤ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወኮነ፤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አመንደቦ ፡ በደዌ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ "ዕፅ ፡ ይብስት"" ፡ ወኮነ“፤ ፡ ጐርባነ ፡ ኅሩየ ፤፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወበጊዜ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ባሞይ ፡ አስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ለአረጋዊያን ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፤ ወመጠወ" ፡ ሎሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ንሥእዎ ፡ "ለዝንቱ ፡ ወዕቀብዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወኢኮነ" ፡ ሰብአ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ !አ ባ ፡ ባሞይ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ እመውት“ ፡ ኅድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፣ ዘተከልከ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ 'ዘሠረፀት ፡ ወፈረየት ፡ እስመ ፡ ለከ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ መሥዋዕት ፡ ቅድስት ፤ ውክፍት ፡ ኅበ?" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ይድኅኑ ፡ በእደዊከ ፣ ወይከውን ፡ ተዝካርከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ "ወሶበ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ባሞይ ፡
Abbä Bämoy was struck by a great illness and remained in this illness for eighteen years. Abbä Yohannes served him and (abbä Bämoy) did not say to him one single day: "You have done well", for this man, abbä Bämoy, was an old man who tested (him) a great deal. God tormented him with a great illness so much that he became like a dried-up (piece of) wood and became a choice offering for God. At the moment of his death, abbä Bämoy gathered the old monks, took the hand of abbä Yohannes and handed (him) over to them, saying: "Take this one and keep him, for he is an angel and he is not a man." Then abbä Bämoy said to abbä Yohannes: "After I have died, dwell in the place where you planted the (piece of) wood that sprouted and bore fruit, for it is for you in this place a holy sacrifice accepted by God. Many people will be saved by your hands and your memory will be in this place forever before God." When he had said this to him, abbä Bämoy opened his mouth and returned his soul into the hand of God.
ዘንተ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ወመጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር" ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወኀደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ?- ፡ አቡሁ ፡ አባ ፡ ባሞይ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ በገቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወተለዓለ” ፡ ስሙ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ ተሠይመ”" ፡ አበ ፡ ም]ኔት ፡ ላዕለ?" ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፤ ዚአሁ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ያነብር ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ቃል ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ይሰምዕዎ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብ ል” ፡ አክስዮስ ፡ አክስዮስ ፡፤ አክስዮስ ። ወዝብሂል ፡ ይደልዎ ፡ ይደልዎ ፡ ይደልዎ ። ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቄድስ ፡ ሦርባነ ፡ ይሬኢ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ይደልዎ ነሚአ ፡ ርባን ፡ ወለዘ ፡ ኢ]ይደልዎ ።
After that, abbä Yohannes went and dwelt in this place according to what his (spiritual) father abbä Bämoy had ordered him. He led a (spiritual) combat by doing what is right and his name was very exalted. After that, he was consecrated abbot of his church; while the patriarch was laying his hand upon him at the moment of his consecration, a voice came from heaven and all the people heard it saying: "Aksyos, aksyos, aksyos", which means "He is worthy, he is worthy, he is worthy". Each time he consecrated the eucharist, the saint Yohannes saw who was worthy to receive the eucharist and who was not worthy (of it).
ወኮነ ፡ አባ ፡ ቴዎፍሎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ሐነጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፣ በስሞሙ" ፡ ለ፻ደቂቅ ፡ ቅዱሳን፤፤ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽእ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ለ፻ደቂቅ ፤ እምሀገረ ፤ ባቢሎን ። ወሰአሎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡ ወያምጽእ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ስአለት ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወፅ አ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ጾረቶ ፡ ደመና ፡ ወአብጽሐቶ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡ ወርእያ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወለአፍላጋቲሃ ፡ ወአጽርሕታ ፡ ወርእ የ ፤ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ለዞደቂቅ ፡ ወሥጋሁ ፡ ለናቡክድናጾር ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እንዘ ፡
Abbä Tëwoflos (= Theophilus) the patriarch had built a church in the name of the three holy children in the city of Alexandria. He wanted to bring the bodies of the three children from the city of Bäbilon (= Babylon). He asked the saint abbä Yohannes to go to the city of Babylon and bring back their bodies. After many instances, abbä Yohannes went out from the patriarch. Immediately, a cloud carried him and brought him to the city of Bäbilon. He saw this city, its rivers and its palaces and he saw the bodies of the three holy children and the body of King Näbukednäsor (= Nebuchadnezzar) placed at their side. The saint abbä
ይነብር ፤ በገቦሆሙ ። ወሰገደ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፣ ወተአምኃ ፡ ሥጋሆ ሙ ፡ ቅዱሳተ ፡ በአንብዕ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸርኅ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኦአበዊየ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ባርኩ ፡ ላዕሌ የ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሰምዓ ፣ ቃለ ፡ እምሥጋሆሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ 'እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ይባርከ5 ።
Yohannes prostrated himself and kissed their holy bodies with weeping while crying out, and he said: "My holy fathers, bless me." Immediately, he heard a voice (coming) from the body of the saints saying: "The Lord God of Israel blesses you."
ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡፣ መልአ ፡ እመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ በበረከት ፡ መንፈሳዊት ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኦአበዊየ5 ፡ ሰማዕታቲሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ" ፡ 'አምላክ ፡ እብለክሙ፤ ፡ እስመ'፡ አቡነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ”፡ ፡ ቴዎፍሎስ ፡ ሐነጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ "በስመ ፣ ዚአክሙ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወፈነወኒ ፡ ኀቤክሙ ፡ ወይስእለክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትብጽሑ ፡ ወትኅድሩ፣ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ” ፡ ቤተ ፡ "ክርስቲያን ፡ በሥጋክሙ" ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ፈው ሰ ፡ ወመድኃኒተ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፣ በኩሉ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወአውሥእአዎ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ በ፳ቃል ፡፤ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ የሀብከ ፡ 'ዕሜተ ፣፤ ጻማከ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። አላ ፡ በሎ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያፍልሱ ፡ ሥጋነ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለ ተ ፡ ትንሣኤ ። አላ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተጋድሎቱ ፡ ወጻጸማሁ ፡ ወሃይማኖቱ ፡ ፍጽምት ፡ ይስቅ ሉ ፡ ማኅዋተ፤" ፡ ውስተ፤፤ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወይግበሩ ፤ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ቅብአ ፡ ወፈትለ ፡ ጡጥ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ያህትውዎሙ ፡ "በሌሊት ፡ በዕለተ፤"። ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቤ ተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ጉቡኣን ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወካህናት ፡ ወንሕነኒ ፡ ንበጽሕ ፡
When abbä Yohannes heard this word, he was filled with the Holy Spirit and rejoiced in the spiritual blessing. He said to them (= to the bodies): "My fathers, martyrs of Christ God, I tell you that our father, the patriarch abbä Tëwoflos, has built a church in your name in the city of Alexandria, has sent me to you and asks you to come and dwell in this church with your holy bodies so that healing and salvation may come to all those who believe in Christ in the whole land of Egypt." The saints answered him with a single voice and said to him: "May God give you the reward of your toil in eternal life. But tell the patriarch that God has prescribed not to transfer our bodies from this place until the day of the Resurrection. But, because of his (spiritual) combat, his toil and his perfect faith, let lamps be hung in the church, let oil and a cotton wick be put there without lighting them on the night of the day of the consecration of the church, all the people and the priests being gathered. We, we will come and reveal the power of God and his blessing."
ወንከሥት!0 ፡ ኀይለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወበረከቶ ። ወሰገደ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ቅዱስ፤" ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፣ ወወፅአ!! ፡ እምኀቤሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትፌሣሕ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር!፤' ። ወሶበ ፡ ርሕቀ ፡ እምኅኀቤሆሙ!፤‹” ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ጾረቶ ፡ ደመና ፡ ወአብጽሐቶ፤" ፡ ኅበ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይቤልዎ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዝ ዎ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ አስተርአ ዩ ፡ ጀደቂቅ ፣፡ ቅዱሳን፤” ፡ በብርሃን፤፤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአብር ሁ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ማኅዋተ፤፤፤ ፡ ወተጹነወ!" ፡ ጹና ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ፈድፋደ ።
The saint abbä Yohannes prostrated himself before them and went out from them rejoicing in God. When he had moved a little away from them, a cloud carried him and brought him to the outside of the city of Alexandria. He entered to the patriarch and told him how the saints had spoken to him. The patriarch did according to what they had ordered him. When it was the middle of the night, the day of the consecration of the church, the three holy children appeared in a great light in the church, they lit all the lamps and a very sweet perfume was exhaled.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ ተመይጠ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ውስተ፤! ፡ ደብሩ፤! ፡ 'ኀበ ፣ አስቄጥስ፤፤ ፡ ወኮነ፤፤ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ በገቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዓልተ፤፤ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ቦአ፤፤ ፡ 8መነኮስ ፤ ውስተ ፡ በዓቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰክብ ፡ ወመላእክተ፤! ፡ እግዚአብሔር፤፤" ፡ ያንዘፈዝፉ፤፤፤ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወለለ ፡ ፳እምኔሆሙ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ኅድጉኒ ፡ ኣንብር ፡ ክንፍየ ፡ ዲቤሁ፤፡፤ ።
After that, abbä Yohannes returned to his convent in 'Asqêtes; he led a (spiritual) combat by doing what is right day and night. One day, a monk entered the cell of abbä Yohannes and found him lying down; the angels of God were spreading their wings above him and each of them was saying to the others: "Let me put my wing on him."
ወእ[ም]ድኅረዝ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ በርባር ፡ አረማዊያን!፤፤" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያመዝብሩ ፡ አድባራተ!፤ ፡ ወይቅትልዎሙ፤፤ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ። ወበእንተዝ፤ ፡ ሖረ ፣ ቅዱስ!" ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ፤፤ ፡ በገዳመ ፡
After this, pagan brigands came to the desert of Scetis to destroy the monasteries and kill the monks. Because of this, the holy abba Yohannes went to the mountain of the holy abba Antony in the

ቀ-ልዝም ። አኮ ፡ ዘይፈርህ ፡ 'እምነ ፡ ሞት፤። ፡ አላ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይምጻእ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ አረሚ ፡ ይቅትለኒ፤ ፡ ወኢይሖር፡፤ ፡ በእንቲአየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ። ወኢይፈቅድ ፡ አነ፤። ፡ ከመ ፡ እኩን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፅረፍት ፤ ወውእቱ ፡ አረሚ፤ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዙነኔ፤ ፡ በእንቲአየ ። ወእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይትቃረነኒ ፡ በአምልኮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ሰ ፡ እጉየ፤ ፡ በፍጥረት ፡ በአርአያ፤ ፡ ወአምሳል ።
desert of Kolzoum, not because he feared death, but he said: "(I am going there) so that a pagan may not come to kill me and go to hell because of me. I do not want to be in rest while the pagan is in punishment because of me. If he opposes me in the worship of God, he is my brother by creation, by appearance, and by likeness."
ወኀደረ ፡ ቅዱስ"' ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ በገቦሃ ፣ ለዓጸድ ፣ ወአምጽአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡፣ እግዚአብሔር!፤፤ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ መሃይምን ። ወኮነ ፡ ይትለአኮ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዕረፍቱ፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርፎ!0 ፣ እምጻማ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኃላ ፊ!፤ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ 'ቅዱሳኒሁ ፡ ጸድቃነ፤። ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ፤ ፡ ወአባ ፡ መቃርስ፤ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይናዝዝዎ ፣ ወያጠይቅዎ ፡ ለፍልሰቱ፤ ። ወኮነ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ አባ ፡ ባሞይ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ይናዝዝዎ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ጽናዕ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተፈሣሕ ፡ በፍሥሐ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ዘዓስተዳለወ ፡ ለከ፤ ። ወኩን ፡ ድልወ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ንመጽእ፤" ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ በጽባሕ ፡ በእለተ ፡ እሑድ ፤ ወንነሥአከ ፡ ኅቤነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕይወት፤ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወባረኩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወተኃብኡ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወበዕለተ ፡ ዓርብ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ለላእኩ ፤ ኅበ ፡ ዓጸድ ፡ ወደወየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ደዌ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ንቅወተ ፡ ዶርሆ ፣ በሌሊ ት ፡ ዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ፡ 'ቅዱስ ፡ ወናሁ! ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እምሳራዊተ፤፥ ፡ መላእክት ፤ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮሙ፤፤፤ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡፤ ወመጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ "ውስተ ፣ እዴሁ፤" ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተቀበሉ" ፡ ቅዱሳን ፤ ወመላእክት፤ ፡ ነፍሶ! ፣ ለብፁዕ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወአዕረግዋ ፡
The holy abba Yohannes stayed next to a village, and God brought him a believing man (who) served him until the moment of his death. When God willed to give him rest from the toil of this transitory world, He sent to him his holy righteous ones, abba Antony, abba Macarius, and abba Pachomius, to comfort him and inform him of his departure. With them was abba Bamoy, his spiritual father. They comforted him and said to him: "Be firm in God and rejoice in the eternal joy He has prepared for you. Be ready, for we shall come to you on Sunday morning and take you with us into eternal life, as God has commanded (us)." The saints blessed him and vanished from his (sight). On Friday, the saint sent his servant to the village and fell slightly ill. When it was the (time of) the cockcrow, on the night of the holy Sunday, behold, many legions of angels and the whole assembly of the saints came to him. When he saw them, he rejoiced, prostrated himself, and committed his soul into the hand of God. The saints and the angels received the soul of the blessed abba Yohannes and took it up with them to heaven.
ምስሌሆሙ፤፤። ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ላእኩ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ "ተዓርግ ፡ ሰማየ፤57-፤፡ጾ ፡ ወቅዱሳን ፤ ወመላእክት፤-!0 ፡ የአውድዋ ፡ ወይዜምሩ ፡ ቅድሜሃ ። ወበቅድመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ፳እንዘ ፡ ይበርህ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ውእቱ!፤! ፡ ይዜምር ፡፤ ወይዌድሶ ፡ ለብፁዕ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ። ወቆመ ፡ መጠነ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡እንዘ ፡ ያነክር፣(፣ በእንተ ፡ ዘርእየ፤" ። ወሶቤሃ ፣፤ ወጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፤ መልአከ ፣ እግዚአብሔር!" ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ ስሞሙ ፡ ለለ ፳፳እምነ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወኮነ ፡ "የአምር ፡ በአጸብዒሁ፤" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዝአባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ወዝአባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወዝአባ ፡ ባሞይ ፡ ወዝአ ባ ፡ እገሌ! ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ላእክ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ወመኑ ፡ ውእቱ! ፡ በቅድሜሆሙ!‹ ፡ ዘይበርህ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐይ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ!” ፡ አበ ፡
At that moment, his servant arrived and saw the soul of the saint ascending to heaven, and the saints and the angels were surrounding it and singing hymns before it. Before all, (there was) someone who shone like the sun; he was singing hymns and praising the blessed abba Yohannes. (The servant) stood for an hour, marveling at what he saw. Then an angel of God came to him and taught him the name of each of the saints; he pointed out the saints to him with his fingers and said to him: "This one (is) abba Pachomius, this one (is) abba Macarius, this one (is) abba Bamoy, this one (is) abba So-and-so." The servant said to the angel: "Who is the one before them who shines like the sun?" (The angel) said to him: "It is abba Antony, the father of all monks."
ኩሎሙ ፡፣ መነኮሳት ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ላእክ ፡ ኅበ ፡ በዓት ፡ ረከቦ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰግድ፤”" ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ "ብካየ ፡ ዓቢየ፤?፤-!"2 ። ወአፍጠነ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዓጸድ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ! ፡ ወመጽኡ፤” ፡፤ወገነዝዎ ፡ ወጾሩ ፡
When the servant arrived at the cell, he found abba Yohannes prostrate. He shed great tears over him and hurried to the village. He told the people how the saint had died. They came, buried him, and carried his

body with great honors. When they brought it into the village, great miracles and wonders occurred from his body. After that, his (spiritual) children the monks arrived, took his body, brought it to the desert of Scetis, and deposited it in his church. It became a haven for whoever took refuge with him. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. On this day also died the holy prophet Hosea, whose name (means) in translation "savior, protector, and king." This righteous man was from the village whose name (is) Alamot, of the tribe of Issachar. He prophesied many prophecies about the children of Israel. He also prophesied and said: "God will descend to earth and will walk with men." He gave a sign of this and said: "The sun will set (far) from Shiloh (?) and will separate into two parts. Twelve oaks will be useful to God, obeying the pure God who will descend to earth. Through Him the whole earth will be saved." When he had finished his prophecy, he died in peace and was buried in his land. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

### READING FOR THE 21ST OF TEQEMT

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day (is) the commemoration of the feast of our Lady, the holy Virgin Mary, Mother of God. May her prayer, her blessing, and her intercession be with us, amen.

On this day also (is) the commemoration of the translation of the body of the holy Lazarus, whom our Lord raised from the dead. One of the Christian emperors (had) his body transferred to the city of Constantinople when he learned his story, namely that he was on the island of Cyprus. When they wanted to transfer him, they found his holy body buried under the earth, placed in a magnificent stone sarcophagus; (it was) written upon it in gold (characters): "This (is) the body of the holy Lazarus, the friend of our Lord Jesus Christ who raised him from the dead after he had remained in the tomb for four days." When (the people) found him, they rejoiced with great joy and carried him to the city of Constantinople. The priests went out, welcomed him with great honors, hymns, and chants, with prayers and incense, and placed him in a sanctuary until a church could be built. They transferred his body, placed it there,

and held a great feast for him like this day. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. [On this day also died the holy abba Yohannes (John), patriarch of the holy city of Jerusalem, in the time of the God-loving king David, (grand)son of King Sayfa Arad. This father was the son of God-fearing Christian parents, (among) the notables of the city of Cairo. They raised this saint and called him by the name Yohannes. When he had grown up, they taught him the psalms of David and the doctrine of the Church. When he was worthy of the diaconate, he was ordained a deacon. When he had well fulfilled the duties of the diaconal service, he was then worthy of the priesthood and was ordained a priest. He was assiduous in prayer, and the fear of God and the love of pilgrims (were) in his heart. Then he was worthy of the "apostolic rank" and, by the will of God, he was appointed patriarch in the holy city of Jerusalem. He waged a beautiful (spiritual) combat there and instructed his people in humility and in the fear of God. The people loved him much for his holiness, his beautiful appearance, and the openness of his mouth (which was) like (that of) an angel of God.
ሁ ፡ ወሠናይ ፡ መልክዑ ፡ ወመክሥተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ መንበሩ ፡ ኅዳጠ ፥ ዓመታተ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መከ ራ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ እስከ ፡ አርሐቆ ፡ እምሕዝቡ ፡ ወእምሀገሩ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በውእ ቱ! ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘለእስክንድርያ ፡ አባ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ አገበሮ ፡ ንጉሠ" ፡ ሐነፋዊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈኑ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ በትእዛዙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተንባላት ፡ እለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ በታሕተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ። እስመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሰይፈ ፡ አርዓድ ፡ ወይሠመይ፤ ፡ ዓዲ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቀተለ ፡ እምነ ፡ ተንባላት ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ዓለውዎ ። ወቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ሰደዶሙ፣ እምኅቤሁ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ 'ለንጉሠ ፡ ምስር ፡ ወተማኅፀኑ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ እስመ”፡ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ አሕለቆሙ ፡ ለተንባላት ፡ ቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ቀተሎሙ ፡ ወቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ረሰዮ
After he had remained for some years on his throne, Satan brought a trial upon him by distancing him from his people and his city. This took place during the patriarchate of our father, the patriarch of Alexandria, abba Mark. The Hanafi sultan forced him to send an embassy to the king of Ethiopia (who was) under his command regarding the Muslims who lived under his royal authority. There was at that time a king whose name (was) Sayfa Arad; he was also called Constantine because he had killed many Muslims when they had rebelled against him. There were some whom he had driven away from him, and they had gone to the city of the sultan of Cairo, taken refuge (there), and said: "The king of Ethiopia has destroyed the Muslims: there are some he has killed, there are some he has made Christians."
ሙ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ምስር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ቀንዓ ፡ ለሃይማኖቱ ፡ ወአገበሮ ፡ ለሊ ቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈኑ ፡ ልሂቃነ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ምሕፅነታ”፤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ በትእዛዝ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እለ ፡ ተርፉ ፡ እምነ ፡ ተንባላት ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ። ወተሰነአወ”" ፡ በምክር ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆኦሳቲሁ ፤ ወመኳንንተ ፡ ምድር?" ፡ በእንተ ፡ እለ ፡ ይፌንውዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ወኃረዩ ፡ እሎንተ ፡ አበወ ፡ ከዋክብተ ፡ ብሩሃነ ፡ እንበይነ ፡ ቅድስናሆሙ ፡ ወአእምሮቶሙ ፡ ወጥበቦሙ ፡ ዘውእቶሙ ፡ አብ ፡ክቡር ፡አባ ፡ዮሐንስ፣ ወአብ፤ ፡ ማእምር ፡ ወለባዊ ፡ አባ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አሕናስ ፡ ወአገበርዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዶሙ ።
When the sultan of Cairo heard these words, he was seized with zeal for his religion and forced the patriarch to send dignitaries from him and informed him (that he considered him) as a hostage regarding the king of Ethiopia, (who was) under (his) command, concerning the Muslims who remained in his kingdom. Our father the patriarch agreed in his view with his bishops and the governors of the country regarding those who would be sent to the king of Ethiopia. They chose these fathers, luminous stars, for their holiness, their knowledge, and their wisdom; they are the illustrious father abba Yohannes and the learned and skillful father abba Cyril, bishop of the city of Behnesa (?). They were forced to go to the king of Ethiopia against their will.


[p. 122] {#po.44.p122}
ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ መፍቀ ሬ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሰይፈ ፡ አርዓድ ፡ ያዕርፍ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ አሜን ። ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ 'ወዓልያኒሁ ፡ ኅቤሆሙ፥፤ ፡ ወምስሌሆሙ ፡ አምኃ ፡ ወአብቅልት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትቀበልዎሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወአክበሮሙ ፡ ክብራተ ፡ ፍድፉዳተ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አንበቡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወኃሠጮ ፡ ገቢአ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መናብርቲሆሙ ፡፤ ወሕዝቦሙ ፡ ወኅበ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናቲሆሙ ፡ ከልኦሙ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡እምነ፡፣ ሐዊር ፡ እንበይነ ፡ ዘአፍቀረ ፡ ቅድስናሆሙ ፡ ወእንበይነ ፤ አፍቅሮቱ ፡ ኪያሆሙ፥፡ ፡ ዘምሉዕ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ እስመ ፡ ከመ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ወይበርሁ ፡ ከመ፣ ፀሐይ ። ወአፍቀሮሙ ፡፣ እግዚእ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቅድስናሆሙ ፡
When they arrived at the border of Ethiopia, the God-loving king Sayfa Arad—may God grant rest to his soul, amen!—heard (of) them, rejoiced with great joy, and sent his soldiers—with them (there were) gifts and mules—to welcome them. When they arrived to the king, (the latter) rejoiced at their (coming) and rendered them great honors. After having read the letter of our father the patriarch to him, (the bishops) asked to return to their throne, their people, and their churches. The lord king forbade them to leave because he loved their holiness and because of his love for them who (were) full of grace; indeed, they were like angels of God and they shone like the sun. The lord king loved them with a great love because of their holiness and their prayer.
ወጸሎቶሙ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ ዘመን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በምሕረቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያፍልሶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እምጻማ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኃላፊ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በርስዕ ፣ ሠናይ ፡ መሥመሬ ፡ ከዊኖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በ፲፻ ወ፪፻ ወ፪ዓመት ፡ እምዓመተ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ንጹሓን ። በረከ ተ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ይዕቀቦ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡
A short time later, when God in His mercy willed to make this father Yohannes leave the toil of this transitory world, He brought a small illness upon him, and he died in a beautiful old age, being pleasing to God, in the one thousand two hundred and seventh year of the era of the pure martyrs. May the blessing of his God guard our king Iyasu with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus in the ages of ages.]
ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ "ነቢይ ፡ ዓቢይ፥ ፡ ኢዩኤል ። ዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለአብያ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኢየሮበዓም ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ወመሀሮሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ወገሠጾሙ ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኅድረቱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በጽዮን ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ሕማማቲሁ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ርደቱ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ንጹሓን ፡ በዕለተ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ዛ ። ወከሠተ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይትነበዩ-9 ወውሉዶመፊ፤ ፡ ወአዕሩጊሆሙ ፡ ወወራዙቲሆሙቶ0። ፡ ወአንስት ፡ እለ ፡ ይነብራ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ። ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በትንቢቱ ፡ አነ ፡ እጐሥዕ ፡ እመንፈስየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወኢጐሥዓ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ላዕለ" ፡ ሐዋርያት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፪ትርጓሜ ፡ ፳ትርጓሜ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ በእንተ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኮ ኑ ፡ ፍጹማነ ፡ በገቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ሰመዮሙ ፡ ሰብአ ። 'እስመ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡ በገቢረ ፥ ሥምረቱ ፡፤ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ኢይሰመይ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወዳግም ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ እስመ ፡ እምነ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ጐሥዓ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዙሎሙ"፣ መሃይምናን ፡ 'እምጊዜ ፡ ስብከቶሙ5፤ ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ፅለተ ፡ ትንሣኤ ።
On this day also died the great prophet Joel. This righteous man prophesied in the time of Abiyya, son of Jeroboam, son of King Solomon. He taught the people and admonished them. He prophesied about the dwelling of God in Zion, about His passion, about the descent of the Holy Spirit, the Paraclete, upon the pure apostles on the day of the feast of Pentecost. He revealed that they (= the people) would prophesy, as well as their children, their old men, their young men, and the women who lived with them. If the prophet said in his prophecy: "I will pour out my spirit upon men" and it only poured out upon the apostles, there are two explanations for this; the first explanation is that (the prophet) called the holy apostles "men" because they were perfect in the practice of justice, for he who is not perfect among men by doing the will of God cannot be called "man." The second explanation is that, since the apostles, the Holy Spirit has poured out upon all believers from the moment of their preaching until the day of the Resurrection.
ወተነበየ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፀአተ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ እምጽዮን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ይውሕዝ ፡ ነቅዕ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወያሰቲ፤፤ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ሳጢም ። ወከሠተ ፡፤ ከመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ይትነሣእ ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድ ር። ወተናገረ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ትንሣኤ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ትጸልም ፡ ወወር
This prophet prophesied about the coming forth of the law of the Gospel from Zion when he said: A fountain will spring forth from the house of God and will water the valley of Shittim. He revealed that, after the coming of our Lord Christ, war would rise upon the earth. He spoke of the day of the Resurrection and


[p. 124] {#po.44.p124}
ኅ ፡ትከውን ፡"ከመ ፡ ደም ፡ ወ[ለ]ከዋክብትኒ ፡ ይትኃባእ ፡ ብርሃኖሙ ። ወተነበየ0፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ" ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በዘ ፡ ይፈደፍድ ፡ እም፲፻ ዓመት ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ እምነገደ ፡ ሮቤል ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ርሥእና ፡ ሠናይት ፡ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአፅረፈ ፡ ወተቀበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ገራህቱ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፤ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ"" ፡ ገብረት ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ተአምረ ፤ ወአድኃነ ቶ ፡ ለማትያስ፤ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገብራ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
said: The sun will be darkened, the moon will become like blood, and, as for the stars, their light will hide itself. He prophesied more than a thousand years before the coming of our Lord Christ. This righteous man was of the tribe of Reuben. He reached a beautiful old age and pleased God. He died and was buried in his field. May his prayer and his blessing be with us in the ages of ages, amen. [On this day also our Lady Mary performed a miracle and saved Matthew from prison. May her prayer and her blessing be with her servant, our king Iyasu, and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus in the ages of ages.]

### READING FOR THE 22ND OF TEQEMT
አመ ፡ ፳፪ለጥቅምት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'ሉቃስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ፤ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱ ስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምኖልቆሙ ፡ ለድወ፪አርድእት ፡ ዘዘከሮሙ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወኮነ ፤ ይትለአኮሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ "ጴጥሮስ ፡ ወጳውሎስ" ፡ ወይጽሕፍ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ እረፍቶሙ ፡ ለጴጥሮስ ፤ ወጳውሎስ ፡ "ኮነ ፡ ሉቃስ ፡ ይሰብክ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ሮሜ ። ወተሰናአዉ ፡ እለ ፡ ያመልኩ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ምስለ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወቆሙ፣ ቅድመ ፡ ኔሮን ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወጸርጥ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሉቃስ ፡ ረድእ ፡፣ ወአስተዋደይዎ ፣ ወይቤሉ” ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አብኦሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ትምህርቱ ፤ ወሥራዩ ። ወአዘዘ"" ፡ ኔሮን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽእዎ ፡ ለሉቃስ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመረ፣ ሐዋርያ፤!! ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይፈልስ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ረከበ ፡ ብእሴ!" ፡ አረጋዌ ፤ ዘያሰግር ፤ ዓሣ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ወጦማራተ ፡ እለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወይቤ ሎ ፡ ዕቀብ፤ ፡ እሉ፤ ፡ መጸሕፍተ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይበቀዑከ ፡ ወያበጽሑከ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day the holy Luke, the evangelist, the physician, became a martyr. This saint was among the seventy-two disciples mentioned by the holy Gospel. He served the apostles Peter and Paul and wrote their history. After the death of Peter and Paul, Luke preached in the land of Rome. Those who worshiped idols conspired with the Jews. They stood before the emperor Nero, cried out because of Luke the disciple, accused him, and said that he had brought many people into his doctrine and his magic. The emperor Nero ordered Luke to be brought. When the apostle knew that he was going to leave this world, he found an old man who was fishing for fish; he gave him the books and the scrolls that were with him and said to him: "Keep these books, for they will be useful to you and will lead you on the path of God."
ውስተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ! ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኔሮን ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ንጉ ሥ ፡ እስከ፤ ፡ ማእዜኑ ፡ አንተ ፡ ታስሕቶሙ ፡ "ለሰብእ ፡ በሥራይከ፤ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሉቃስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢኮንኩ፤፤ ፡ መሠርይ ፡ አላ ፡ አነ ፡ ሐዋርያሁ ፡ ለእግዚእ፤ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡፤ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ። ወአዘዘ" ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ዘየማን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አነ ፡ እመትራ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ እድ ፡ ዘኮነት ፡ ትጽሕፍ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ መተሮ፤፤ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሉቃስ ፡ ንሕነ ሰ ፡ "በአማን ፡ ኢንፈርህ።። ፡ እሞተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ። አላ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ትሬኢ፡፡ ፣ ኀይሎ ፡ ለኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወሰፍሐ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ዘፀጋም ፡ ወነሥኦ ፡ ለእዴሁ ፡ ዘየማን ፣፡ ዘተመትረት ፣ወአልጸቃ?" ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካና ፡ ወለጸቀት ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ከመ ፡ ካልእታ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈለጣ ፡ ወተፈልጠት ።
When (Luke) had arrived before Nero, the emperor of Rome, and stood before him, the emperor said to him: "How long will you deceive people with your magic?" The holy Luke answered and said to him: "I am not a magician, but I am an apostle of our Lord Jesus Christ, the Son of the living God." The emperor ordered his right hand to be cut off, saying: "I (will) have this hand cut off that wrote books." Immediately, he (had) his hand cut off. The holy Luke said to him: "We, in truth, do not fear the death of this world, but behold, now you are going to see the power of Jesus Christ." The saint stretched out his left hand, took his right hand that had been cut off, applied it to its place, and it adhered and was like the other; then he separated it, and it was separated.


[p. 126] {#po.44.p126}
ወአንከሩ” ፡ እለ ፡ ነበሩ" ፡ በህየ ፡ ወአምነ፤ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ወብእሲቱ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወኮነ ፣ ኖልቆሙ ፡ ፪፻፳ ወ5 ነፍስ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ፥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ፤ርእሶሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ሐዋርያ፣ ሉቃስ-3፤ወመተሩ ፤ ርእሶሙ!፥ ፣ ለኩሎሙ?" ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ሰማያት ። ወረሰዩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ሉቃስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ሠቀ ፣ ጸጐር ፤ ወወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ። ወበፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አብጽሖ ፡ መዋግደ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ኀበ፥ ፡ ደሴት" ። ወረከቦ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ መሃይምን ፡ ወነሥኦ ፡ ወገነዞ ፡ "በመግነዝት ፡ሠናይት፤ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ወንጌሎ ፡ ለቴዎፍሎስ። ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምነ ፡ አሕዛብ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ሐዋርያት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ሉቃስ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
Those who were there marveled, and the chief of the soldiers and his wife believed, and many people believed in our Lord Jesus Christ. Their number was two hundred and seventy-seven people. The emperor ordered their heads to be cut off with the apostle Luke; they cut off all their heads, and they received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. They put the body of the holy apostle Luke in a hair sack and threw it into the sea. By the will of God, the waves of the sea brought it to an island. A believer found it, took it, and buried it in a beautiful shroud. This saint wrote his gospel for Theophilus, who was from among the Gentiles, then he wrote the Acts of the Apostles for him. May the prayer and the blessing of this apostle Luke be with us forever, amen.

<On this day also died the blessed and holy abba Gabra Maryam, doctor of Dabra Zalekhugn. While he was small, he entered this convent and learned to read the Scriptures and to chant (the psalms of) David. After the end of the sleep prayer, when the monks went to sleep, he would enter the church and chant these psalms of David, prostrate until his sweat flowed and dripped onto the ground. He remained doing this for many days, with no one knowing. After he had grown up, he asked the abbot abba Gregory to clothe him in the monastic habit, and (the latter) clothed him (in it). He redoubled his prayer, fasting, and adoration so that his virtue was known to all the holy fathers. When abba Gregory died, he was appointed in his place, taught his (spiritual) sons manual labor, and prescribed to them not to feed themselves in idleness. A woman brought her son to him. From his loins downward, (he was?) like a man. When (Gabra Maryam) had baptized him, he was healed. When a large stone was descending from the top of the mountain, he cursed it, and it stopped. God performed many miracles through his hands. He lived until old age and died in peace. May God have mercy through his prayer on his dear Se'ela Seyon in the ages of ages, amen> READING FOR THE 23RD OF TEQEMT. In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father abba Joseph, patriarch of the city of Alexandria. Among the patriarchs, he is the fifty-second. This father was of the children of the notables of the city of Manouf (or Memphis); (his parents) had much money. When they died (and)

left him an orphan, a God-loving man raised him. When he had grown up and had become an adolescent, he took his money and gave it to the poor and the needy. Then he went up to the desert of Scetis, to the monastery of the holy abba Macarius, and became a monk under a holy old man. When Mark the Second was appointed patriarch and heard about this holy abba Joseph that his character was beautiful, he sent for him, brought him to him, and installed him in his cell. After many days, (Joseph) begged abba Mark the patriarch and said to him: "Let me go to the desert of Scetis." Then (the patriarch) ordained him a priest and sent him to Scetis. He remained there for many years until the father abba Simon, the patriarch, died. The land of Egypt remained without a patriarch for many years after the death of abba Jacob. There were some among the bishops who agreed with a man, a scribe in the imperial house, who had been married, whose wife had died, and who was not a monk. They received bribes from him, and half the people of the city of Alexandria agreed with them to appoint this scribe patriarch. All the bishops stood against them and said: "This plan that you have meditated is not good, but it is evil, for the holy canon excommunicates whoever receives bribes or gives (them) for the priestly dignity; even more (must this be the case), since this man has been married and is not worthy of the patriarchal dignity. Indeed, until now, no one has been consecrated on the chair of Mark the evangelist except a virgin (man)." These (= the simoniac bishops?) joined them in this good opinion.
ምክር ፡ ሠናይ ። ወተንሥኡ ፡ ወቦኡ፥፤” ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ወአስተብሞዑ ፡ ኀ በ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ዘይጌይስ” ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ሚመት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ስእለቶሙ ፡ ወአዝከሮሙ ፡ "በዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፣ ወፈነዉ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽእዎ ፡ እምገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ። ወእለ ፡ ተፈነዉሰ ፡ ጸለዩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ ንስእለከ ፡ እግዚኦ?" ፡ ከመ ፡ ትክሥት ፡ ለነ?" ፡ "ግብረ ፡ ግሱሰ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮንከ" ፡ ኃረይኮ ፡ ለዝንቱ፤ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን። ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ሀበነ ፡ ትእምርተዝ ፡ "ወረስየነ ፡ ንርከብ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ በዓ ቱ ፡ ርኅወ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ "ኅበ ፡ በዓቱ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ ጥፕኅቶ ፡ ርኅወ ። ወረከብዎ ፣ ለአባ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ እንዘ ፡ 'ያስተፋኑ ፡ አሐደ ፡ መነኮሰ" ፣፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወይዕፁ ፡ ኖኅቶ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ተአምኖሙ ፡ በአም ኃ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ወተወክፎሙ ፡ በፍሥሐ ፡ ወአብኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ፤! ። ወሶበ ፡ቦ ኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ። ፡ አኀዝዎ፥ ፡ ወአሰርዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ይደልዎ ፡ ይደልዎ ፡ ይደልዎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፤ ሚመተ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጵጵስና ። ወጸርኃ፤ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡
They rose, entered the church, prayed, and begged God to reveal to them the one who was the best for this dignity. Immediately, God heard their prayer and reminded them of this father abba Joseph. They sent messengers to bring him from the desert of Scetis. Those who had been sent prayed to God and said: "We ask You, Lord, to reveal to us something palpable if You have chosen this abba Joseph to be patriarch; give us this sign: grant us that we find the door of his cell open." When they arrived at his cell, they found his door open. And they found abba Joseph taking leave of a monk, and he wanted to enter his cell and close his door. When he saw them, he embraced them with a holy kiss, welcomed them with joy, and brought them into his house. When they had entered his house, they seized him and bound him, saying: "He is worthy, he is worthy, abba Joseph is worthy of the patriarchal office!" He cried,


[p. 130] {#po.44.p130}
ይቤሎሙ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ገበርኩ ፡ 'ብዙኃ ፡ ኃጢአተ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ፥" ። ወኢተወክፉ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ምክንያተ ። አላ ፡ ወሰድዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ።
wept, and began to say to them: "I have committed many more sins than all men." They did not accept this excuse from him, but they took him to the city of Alexandria and consecrated him patriarch.
ወሶበ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፣ መንበረ ፡ ሚመቱ”" ፡ ሐለየ ፡ በእንተ ፣ አብያተ"! ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ፈድፋዶ ። ኮነ ፡ ዘይተርፍ ፡ እምጸባሕቱ። ፡ ይሣየጥ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ጐልታተ ፡ ምድር ፤ ወይጐልቶሙ ፡ ለአብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ። ወኮነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ወኢይትዓወር ፡ እምነ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ።
When he was seated on the throne of his office, he reflected much about the churches. With what was left of his income, he bought lands and established them as an endowment for the churches. He instructed the people ceaselessly and did not neglect any of them.
ወቀንአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወአመክነየ ፡ ሎቱ" ፡ ምክንያተ ፤ ወአምጽአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሐዘነ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ትኒስ ፡ ወኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦስ" ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ምስር ፣ አሕሰሙ?”" ፡ ሕዝቦ[ሙ]?፤ ፤ በግብርናት ፡ ወአመንደብ ዎሙ?" ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ "ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ተዐገሙ ፡ ወኢታመንድብዎሙ ፡ ለሕዝ ብ]ክሙ ። ወሰአሎሙ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአዩ ፡ መርዔቶሙ ፡ በዓይነ" ፡ ምሕረት ። ወኢተወክፉ ፡ ትእዛዞ ፡ ወኃደጎሙ?" ። ወሶበ ፡ አመንደብዎሙ ፡ እሙን ቱ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ ለሕዝቦሙ”ጾ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ መርዔቶሙ" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሊቀ፤ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ወጸርጉ፤-፤ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢሰአርኮ ሙይ› ፣ ለእሉ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ እምላዕሌነ ፣ ወእመአኮ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ንበውእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ካልእት ፡ ሃይማኖት ። ወተጋደለ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፣፤ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ማእከሌሆሙ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ወኢተክህሎ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አብጽሖሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ እምኩሉ፤ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ኤኢ ስ ፤ ቆፅሳት" ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አመንደቡ ፡ እሉ ፡ ፪ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ለሕዝቦ[ሙ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወአብጽሖሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ፪ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ማእከሌሆሙ ፡ ወማእከለ ፡ ሕዝቦሙ ። ወኢሰምዑ ፡ ትእዛዞ ፣ ወኢትእዛዘ ፤ አኀዊሆሙ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ። ወሶቤሃ ፣፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ሊ ቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ በቅድመ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡፣ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ አነ ፣፡ ንጹሕ ፡ እምኃጢአትክ ሙዖይ‹ ። ወእሉ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆልሳት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙን ቱ ፡ ተአደዉ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ ሊቆሙ፤ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ወጸሐፉ ፡ 'ዙሎሙ ፡
Satan was jealous of him, wove snares against him, and brought afflictions upon him; this (took place) thus: the bishop of the city of Tanis and the bishop of the city of Cairo were acting badly toward their people (by putting them) into servitude and were tormenting them. This father said to them: "Restrain yourselves and do not torment your people." He begged them many times to pasture their flock with a merciful eye. They did not accept his order, and he left them. As these bishops were tormenting their people, all their sheep came to the patriarch abba Joseph, cried out before him, and said to him: "If you do not remove these bishops from over us, we will enter into another faith." Abba Joseph waged a great combat to make peace between them, but did not succeed. After that, he brought all the bishops from the whole land of Egypt, and when the bishops were gathered to him, he told them how these two bishops were tormenting their people. The patriarch sent for the two bishops and wanted to make peace between them and their people. But (the bishops) did not obey his order nor the order of their brothers the bishops. Then the patriarch said to them before all the bishops: "I am innocent of your sin," and all the bishops were witnesses against them that they had transgressed the order of their leader, the patriarch abba Joseph. All wrote with their hand that one should remove these two bishops from their office. Then the patriarch removed them from their office.
በእደዊሆሙ። ፡ ከመ ፡ እሉ ፡ ፪ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትርዎሙ ፡ እምሚመቶምሙ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ መተሮሙ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ እምሚመቶሙ ። ወሐሩ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወአስተዋደይዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ በነገረ ፡ ሐሰት ። ወፈነወ ፡ "ንጉሥ ፡ እጉሁ ፡ ወምስሌሁ”" ፡ ሰራዊተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ” ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ! ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ መልሑ፣ ሰይፎ ፡ እጐሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትሎ ። ወሶበ፤"፤ ፡ ዘበጦ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ አጽነነ ፡ "እግዚአብሔር ፡ እዴሁ!" ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ "ሰይፉ ፡ ኀበ ፣ አምድ፤ ፡ ወተሰብረ፤የ ። ወተቁጥዓ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወመልሐ ፡ ካልአ ፡ መጥባሕተ"" ፡ ወዘበጦ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገቦሁ፤ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ኀይሉ፤' ።
These (bishops) went to the sultan of Egypt and accused this father abba Joseph with lying words. The sultan sent his brother and with him soldiers so that they might bring the patriarch to him. When they arrived to him, the brother of the sultan drew his sword and wanted to kill him. When he (wanted) to strike the patriarch with (his) sword, God turned the hand of this officer, and his sword struck a pillar and broke. He became very angry, drew another sword, and struck the patriarch on the side with all his strength. His clothes


[p. 132] {#po.44.p132}
ወተበትኩ ፡ አልባሲሁ ፡ ወቅናቱ ፡ ወኢበጽሐ ፡ መጥባሕት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለሊ ቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ። 'ወጠየቀ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት፤-፤0 ፡ ጸድቅ፤!፤' ፡ ወውስቴቱ!!! ፡ ጸጋ፤፤፡ ፡ መለኮታዊት ፡ ተዐቅቦ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ወእምቀቲል!! ።
and his belt were cut, but the sword did not reach the body of the patriarch. The officer understood that the patriarch (was) a righteous man and (that) in him (was) a divine grace (that) preserved him from all evil and from murder.
ወአክበሮ፤፤ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እሁ ፡ ንጉሥ፤! ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ክብር ፡ ወነገ ሮ፤፤ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ላዕለ፤፤ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ዘበጦ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ፪ጊዜ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ድኅነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘንተ፤! ፡ ነገረ፤፤ ፡ አንከረ፤፡" ፡ ፈድፋደ፤፡፤ ፡ ወፈርሆ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወአክበሮ ። ወእምዝ ፡ 'ተስእለ ፡ እምኔሁ፤፡፡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እሉ ፡ ፪ኤጺስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ "እለ ፡ አስተዋደይዎ ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ወነገሮ ፡ ሊቀ ፤ ጳጳሳት፤፤፤-፤፡ ፣፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ"፡ ፡ እስተዋደይዎ ፡ በነገረ ፡ ሐሰት ፡ ወአእመረ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ 'ኤኢስ ፡ ቆልሳት፤፤-፤። ፡ ሐሳዊያን ፡ ወአላዊያን ፡ "ርጉማን ፡ ወአማጺያን፤ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ኤኢስ፤፡ ፡ ቆኦሳት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ለንጉሥ፤፤ዖ ፡ ንሕነሰ፤!፤ ፡ አዘዘነ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ፣ ንግበር ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ህየንተ ፣ እኩይ ። 'ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮኑ፤ ፡ አስተዋደዩኒ ፡ ኀቤከ፤ቶ ፡ በሐሰት፤፤ ፡ ናሁ፤፤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር፤ ፡ ወደየ፤፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልብከ ፤ ከመ ፡ አነ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ እምዘ ፡ አስተዋደዩኒ ፡ 'ኀቤከ ፡ ቦቱ፤ ፡ እስእለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትምሀሮሙ ፡ ወ[ት]ኅድጎሙ ፡ ሊተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ የውሃቶ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አንከረ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ አክበሮ ፡ ወመሀረ ፡ ሎቱ፤" ፡ ፤፪ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳተ ። ወጸሐፈ!፤-!። ፡ ጦማረ ፡ ወወሀቦ! ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትቃረንዎ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ 'ኢሥዩማን ፡ ወኢ[ኤ]ጺሏስ ፡ ቆጳሳት፤ ፡
He honored him and brought him to his brother the sultan with great honors. He told him (= his brother) how he had acted against the patriarch, how he had struck him with the sword twice, and how he had (remained) unharmed. When the sultan heard these words, he was very astonished, was afraid of the patriarch, and honored him. Then he questioned him about the two bishops who had accused him to him. The patriarch told him what the situation was with them and demonstrated to him that they had accused him with lying words. The sultan understood that these bishops (were) liars and cursed and dishonest impious men. Immediately, the sultan ordered these bishops to be killed. The patriarch said to the sultan: "To us, our Lord Christ has prescribed in the holy Gospel to do good for evil. If they have accused me to you falsely, behold, God has put into your heart that I was innocent of what they accused me of to you. I ask you to be merciful toward them and to leave them for me for the sake of God." When the sultan saw the clemency of the patriarch, he marveled much, honored him, and spared the two bishops for him. He wrote a decree and gave (it) to him. He prescribed in it that neither the officials nor the bishops should resist the patriarch nor transgress his order and what he would want to do in his office, whether it be appointing or whether it be dismissing.
ወኢይትዓደዉ ፡ ትእዛዞ! ፡ ወዘፈቀደ፤ ፡ ይግበር ፡ በሚመቱ ፡ እመሂ ፡ ይሠይም ፤ ወእመሂ ፡ ይሥዕር ። ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ፈነወ፤ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ መልእክተ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አንሰ ፡ እሰግድ ፡ ለመንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘይደልወከ፤ ፣ ከመ ፡ ትንበር ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ወበጸጋሁ ፡ ጸንዓ! ፡፣ መንግሥትየ ። ወእስእለከ፤ቦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሣሀል ፡ ላዕሌነ፤፤ ፡ ወትፈኑ፤። ፡ ለነ ፡ ጳጳሰነ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐን ስ ፡ እስመቦ ፡ እምነ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገርነ! ፡ ሥሕቱ ፡ እምብርሃነ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ለማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወረሰዩ ፡ እገሪሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ እንተ ፡ ምልእት ፡ሦክ ፡!ሶ በ ፡ ሰደድዎ፤ ፡ ለጳጳስነ ፡ አባ ፣ ዮሐንስ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አምጽአ፤፤ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር፤ ፡ ዓቢየ፤" ፡ ምንዳቤ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔርነ ፡ ወሞቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ፤ ፡ በደዌ ፡ ብድብድ ፡ ወእንስሳ፤ ፡ ሐልቁ ፡ ወከልአ፤፤ ፡ ለሰማይ ፡ ከመ፣ "ኢትዝንም ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ብሔርነ፤ ፡ ወተንሥኡ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ጸላእትነ፤ ፡ ወሞኡነ፤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘተዐደውነ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኦአቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተሣሀል ፡
In the time of this father, the king of Ethiopia sent him a letter saying: "I prostrate myself before the throne of Mark the holy evangelist, upon which it is just that you be seated—and by his grace my kingdom is strong. I ask you to have mercy on us and to send us our metropolitan abba Yohannes, for there are some among the people of our country who have strayed from the light of the throne of Mark the holy evangelist and have set their feet on a path that is full of thorns, when they drove out our metropolitan abba Yohannes. Because of this, God has brought a great misfortune upon us in our country, and all the people have died of a pernicious disease, the animals have perished, and (God) has prevented the sky from letting rain fall upon our country. Our enemies have risen against us and have defeated us because we have transgressed the commandment of God. Now, our holy father,


[p. 134] {#po.44.p134}
"ላዕለ ፡ እበድነ!፥ ፡ ወፈኑ ፡ ለነ ፡ ጳጳሰነ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተብቀ-ዕ! ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይጹሊ'"" ፣ በእንቲአነ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንድኀን ፣ እምዝንቱ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡፤ በጸሎትክሙ!፤ ፡ ወበጸሎቱ ። ወአነ ፡ አጠይቀከ፤”" ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ምክንያተ፤!! ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ወልድከ፤” ፡ ተባረኩ ፡ እምነ ፡ አቡየ' ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ጳጳስ ። ወውእቱ ፡ አስተፋነወኒ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰራዊትየ ፡ ወባረኮሙ፤ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሚመቱ ። ወንሕነኒ ፡ ሖርነ ፡ ኅበ፤” ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ወነበርነ፤ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ በውስተ፤?" ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ወሞኡነ ፡ ጸላእትነ ፡ ወኅልቁ ፡ ሰራዊትነ ፡ ወጐየይነ ፡ ወገባእነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔርነ ። ወኃጣእናሁ ፡ ለጳጳስነ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወሰአልኩ፤ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፣፤ ወነገሩኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብእሲትየ ፡ ንግሥት ፡ ሰደዶደቶ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አምከርዋ፤!”" ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እኩያን ፡ በከመ ፡ ሰደደቶ ፡ አውዶክስያ ፡ ንግሥት ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ አፈ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ በቀዳሚ ፡፣ መዋዕ ል። ወሜሙ ፡ሎሙ ፡ካልአ ፡ጳጳሰ ፡ በፈቃዶሙ ፡ ወተዓደዉ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ቅዱስ ፣፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ጠፍአ ፡ ብሔርነ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ተሣሀል ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ወፈኑ ፡ ለነ!" ፡ ጳጳሰነ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጹሊ'፤፤ ፡ በእንቲአነ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ጳጳስ፤ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰደድዎ ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ወኃደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብሩ ፡ ዘመንኩሰ ፡ ቦቱ ፣ ወውእቱ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ በገዳ መ፤ ፣ አስቄጥስ ። ወሶበ ፡ አንበበ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ መልእክቶ ፡ ለንጉ ሠ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በአሚኖቱ፤ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ፣ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወአብጽሖ፤፤ ፡ ወናዘዞ ፡ ወአጽንዖ ፡ ወፈነዎ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ጽኑዓነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ፤ ፡ ተአተተ ፡ ብድብድ ፡ ወወረደ ፤ ዝናም ፡ እምሰማይ ፣፡ ወተፈሥሑ፤፤ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ በብጽሐቱ ።
have mercy on our folly and send us our metropolitan abba Yohannes so that he may beg God and pray for us so that we may be saved from this misfortune by your prayer and by his prayer. I inform you, my father, of what was the cause (of this): I, your son, was blessed by my father abba Yohannes the metropolitan; he said goodbye to me and to my troops and blessed them, and he returned and remained in his office. As for us, we went to war and remained for many days at war. Our enemies defeated us, our troops perished, and we fled and returned to our country. We did not find our metropolitan abba Yohannes; I asked after him, and I was told that my wife the queen had driven him out when wicked people advised her (to do so)—just as the empress Eudoxia drove out John Golden-Mouth (John Chrysostom) in the days of old. They appointed another metropolitan for themselves of their (own) will, transgressed the commandment of the holy canon, and, because of this, our country has perished. Now, have mercy on us and send us our metropolitan abba Yohannes so that he may pray for us." As for this metropolitan, when they drove him out of the country of Ethiopia, he went and lived in his monastery in which he had become a monk. It is the monastery of the holy abba Moses in the desert of Scetis. When the patriarch abba Joseph read the letter of the king of Ethiopia, he rejoiced much at his trust, sent quickly to the desert of Scetis, brought him (= Yohannes), encouraged him, comforted him, and sent him. He sent courageous men with him to the country of Ethiopia. When (Yohannes) (arrived) there, the plague stopped and rain fell from the sky. They experienced great joy at his arrival.
ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ይጌሥጾሙ ፡ ለኃጥኣን ፡ ወዓላዊያን፤ ፡ ወይመይጦሙ ፡፣ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖቱ፤ ፡ ርትዕት ። ወከማሁ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወኮነ፤ ፡ ያጸንዖሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት፤፤-!፤ ፡ ዘተመጠውዋ ፡ እምአበዊ ሆሙ፤። ፣ ወኮነ ፡ ይተረኾም፤ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ዘሕቡእ ፡ ዘውስተ፤ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ቅዱሳ ት ፡ ወያለብዎሙ፤ ፣ ትርጓሜሆሙ ፡ ወየዐቅቦሙ ፡ በትምህርታቲሁ ፣ ወጸሎታቲ ሁ፤ ። ወከሠተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ዲበ ፡ እደዊሁ፤ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሴ ፍ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፡ ዓበይተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ዝንቱ፤! ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ሠናየ"፡ ፡ ወለኮታዊተ ፤ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕ[ረ]ፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፣ ፲ወ፪ዓመተ ፤ ወበምንዙ ስና።9፤ ፡ ጠወዘ፪ዓመት ፣ ወእምቅድመ ፡ ምንኩስና""" ፥ ፳ዓመት ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡፣
This father rebuked sinners and the impious and converted them to his right faith. Likewise, he strengthened all the Christian people in the right faith which they (= the people) had received from their fathers. He interpreted for them what is hidden in the Holy Scriptures and made them understand their interpretation. He guarded them through his teachings and his prayers. God caused great miracles and wonders to appear through the hands of this holy Abba Yosëf. When he had finished his beautiful divine combat and had pleased God, he died in peace after having remained on the seat of Markos the Evangelist for nineteen years, and in the monastic state for thirty-nine years; before (entering) the monastic state, (he had lived) twenty years. All the days of his life were seventy-eight years. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፤ ድወቿዓመት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዲዮናስዮስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆቆስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፥ ቆሮቶስ። ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ በመዋዕሊሆሙ ፡ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ወመክስምያኖስ ፡!ነገሥት ፣
On this day also the holy Diyonäsyos (= Dionysius), bishop of the city of Qorotos (= Corinth), became a martyr in the time of Diyogletyänos


[p. 136] {#po.44.p136}
ከሓዲያን" ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ኩነንዎ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወሶበ ፣፤ ደክመ” ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ መተረ' ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
(= Diocletian) and Maksemyänos (= Maximian), the impious emperors. They subjected this saint to great torture, and when he (= the executioner?) was weary of torturing him, he cut off his head with a sword, and (the saint) received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May his prayer and his blessing be with us,
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ" ፡ ዕረፍቶሙ ፡ "ለጤንትያቄ ፣ ወቴኤዱስዮስ፤" ፡ ወተላዎስ ፡ ወተኦክሴስ ፡ ወዮሳብ ፡ ወተጋድሎቱ ፡ ለእንድርያስ ፡ ሰማዕት ። በረከተ ፡ አምላኮሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
amen. [On this day is the death of Tënteyägë, Té'édusyos (= Theodosius), Taläwos, Ta'oksës, and Yosäb (= Joseph), and the (spiritual) combat of 'Endreyäs (= Andrew) the martyr. May the blessing of their God be with our king 'Iyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever, amen.]
አመ ፡ ፳፪ለጥቅምት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ "አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ጻድቅ፤ ፣፡ መስተጋድል ፡ አባ ፡ አብላርዮን፤ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ባሕታዊ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ጋዛ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ አረማዊያን ። ወመሀርዎ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ትምህርታተ ፡ ዮናናዊያን ፡ ወጥበቦሙ ፡ ወተለዓለ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በጥበቡ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እምአብያጺሁቶ ። ወኮነ ፡ ለጥበብ ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ነፍሱ ። ወፈተወ ፡ ከመ ፡ያጽንዕፅ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ሠናየ ፡ ካልአ ። ወኢኮነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ዘይምህሮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ወይፈጽም?” ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዘይፈቅድ ። ወተንሥአ" ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይነብሩ!" ፡ መምህራን ፡ ወተምህረ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ትምህርታተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ። ወተሀውከ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ቅንዓት ፤ መለኮታዊት ፡ "ከመ ፡ ይትመሀር፤፤ ፡ ትምህርታተ፤" ፡ ክርስቲያን"" ፡ "ወኀሠሠ ፡ መጸሕፍተ! ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወወሀብዎ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ ወአንበቦሙ ፡ ወለበዎሙ ። ወኮነ ፡ አባ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ፤፤ ፡ ይከሥት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወይተረጉም ፡ ሎቱ፤ ፡ዘኅቡእ ፡ ውስተ፤ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ወያሌብዎ ። ወአምነ፤ ፡ በእግዚእነ፤ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአጥመቆ ፡ አባ፤ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ፣
[READING] FOR 24 TEQEMT. In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy, righteous father, the athlete, Abba 'Abläryon (= Hilarion), the hermit monk. This saint was from the people of the city of Gäzà (= Gaza) and his parents were pagans. They instructed him in all the Greek sciences and their wisdom. He was highly exalted by his wisdom above many of his comrades; there was a dwelling for wisdom in his soul. He desired to learn another beautiful wisdom, but there was no one in his city who could teach him this wisdom and accomplish for him what he wanted. He arose, went to the city of Alexandria, and entered a place where there were professors; he learned many sciences there. A divine zeal stirred in him (and pushed him) to study the sciences of the Christians. He sought the books of the Church, they were given to him, he read them, meditated upon them, and Abba 'Eleskenderos (= Alexander), patriarch of the city of Alexandria, discovered for him and explained what (was) hidden in the books and made him understand (it). He believed in our Lord Christ, and Abba 'Eleskenderos baptized him with Christian baptism.
ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወተጋደለ፡፤ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡፤ ዓቢየ፡፤ ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ ወተፀምደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ "ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ” ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትመሀር ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ጽድቅ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ከመ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ሥጋዊያን ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ዘኀደጉ ፡ ወወሀበ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወምስኪናን ፡ ወለፅኑሳን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ እምአድባራት ፡ዘሀገረ ፡ ሶርያ ። ወተጋደለ ፡'ዓቢየ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ወተፀምደ ፡ ተፀምዶ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወበሰጊድ ፡ ወበትጋህ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይጸውም ፡ ኒመዓልተ፣0
He waged a great (spiritual) combat, became a monk, and was assiduous in the way of the monastic life. Then he went to the holy Abba 'Entonyos and remained with him for many years, learning from him the way of righteousness. When he learned that his parents according to the flesh had died, he went to his city, took the money they had left (him), and gave it to the poor, the indigent, and the needy. Then he entered a monastery among the monasteries of the land of Soryä (= Syria). He waged a great (spiritual) combat and devoted himself to a beautiful asceticism in fasting, prayer, adoration, and vigil. He fasted for seven


[p. 138] {#po.44.p138}
ወዒጌሌሊተ0፥፤ ፡ ወይሴሰይ ፡ ሣዕረ ፡ ዘገዳም ። ወበርሀ ፡ ልቡ፥። ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔ ር ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ትንቢት ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ።
days and seven nights and fed himself on the herbs of the desert. His intelligence shone, God gave him the gift of prophecy, and he performed miracles and wonders.
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፣ ኤሏፋንዮስ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ፤ ፡ ደብር፥ ፡ ወመጠዎ?" ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ፥ጾ ፡ አብላርዮን ፡ ወመሀሮ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወትምህርተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ከርስቲያን፤ ። ወተነበየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ "ውእቱ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆጾስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ ቆጵሮስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ።
After he had remained in this monastery for many years, the holy 'Epifanyos (= Epiphanius) became a monk in this convent, and the superior entrusted him to this holy 'Abläryon (who) taught him monastic science and the science of the Church. ('Abläryon) prophesied concerning him that he would become bishop of the city of Qopros (= Cyprus), and (this) happened as he had prophesied concerning him.
ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ጭዓመትቶ፥ ፡ ፲ዓመት ፡ ውስተ፣ ቤተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ፪ዓመት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ፳ወሾዓመት ፡ "ውስተ ፡ ምንኩስና ፤ በተጋድሎ ። ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ "በርሥዕና ፡፤ ሠናይት ። ወወደሶ ፡ ቅዱስ፥ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ አፈ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ" ። ወካዕ በ ፡ ወደሶ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ፤፤ ፡ በመጸሕፍቲሁ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌ
All the days of this saint were eighty years: ten years in his father's house, seven years in the city of Alexandria, sixty-three years in the monastic life in (spiritual) combat. He pleased God and died in a beautiful old age. The holy Yohannes Golden-Mouth praised him, and the holy Bäselyos (= Basil) also praised him in his writings. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ነ ፡ አሜን። ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ወቢጹ፥። ፡ ሰማዕታት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፤ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ቅድስት፥ ፡ ዘይና ፡ ሰማዕት ። ወተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለአውስያ ፡ ወቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ "ወፀበለ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ተኃራሚት ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፤ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፳፳ለጥቅምት ፡[ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡'አብ ፡ ቅዱስ' ፡ አባ" ፡ አቢብ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወስመ ፡ እሙ ፡ ኃሪካ ፡ እምበሓውርተ ፡ ሮም ፡ ወእምአድያመ ፡ ሉፊ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስደት ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ መክስምያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጐይዩ ፡ እምሀገር ፡ ለሀገር ። ወሶበ ፡ ኃጥኡ ፡ ውሉዶደሩ ፡ ሰአሉ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ። ወአስተርአዮሙ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በሕልም ፡ ወመጠዎሙ ፡ አስካለ ፡ ዘምሉእ ፡ ፍሬ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፀንሠ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በቄለ ፡ በዓውደ ፡ ቤቶሙ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ኦም ፡ ወጽሑፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቀ'ጸሊሁ ፡ በነገረ ፡ ሮማይስጥ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ቡላ” ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወቅዱሱ ፡ ለአምላከ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ዘየኃድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽዮን ።
On this day also the holy Päwlos (= Paul) and his friend were martyrs. May their prayer be with us, amen. On this day also is the holy Zaynä (= Zena) the martyr [and the commemoration of 'Awsyä and Quastantinos (= Constantine) and the dust (?) of Mary the abstinent.] May her prayer and her blessing be with us, amen. [READING] FOR 25 TEQEMT. In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father Abba 'Abib. [The name of the father of this saint was 'Abrehäm (= Abraham) and the name of his mother, Härikä, (they were) from the territories of Rome, from the region of Lufi. When there was persecution in the time of the emperor Maksemyänos (= Maximian), they fled from city to city. As they had no children, they prayed to God in fasting and prayer. An angel of God appeared to them in a dream and handed them a cluster of grapes that (was) full of fruit. When the saint was conceived, a great tree grew in the courtyard of their house. It was written on its leaves in the Latin language these words: "Bulä, servant of God and saint of the God of Jacob who dwells in Seyon (= Zion)".
ወሶበ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ አንበርዎ ፡ ፳ዓመተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ያብእዎ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወእምዝ ፡ አስተርአየቶ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሮሜ ፡ ወአዘዘቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖ
When he was born, they left him for a year without having him enter Christianity. Then our Lady Mary appeared to the patriarch of Rome and


[p. 140] {#po.44.p140}
ር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወያጥምቆ ፡ ለሕፃን ። ወበጺሖ ፡ አብኦ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ሰመዮ፣0፣ ቡላ ። ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ አስተዓፀቡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘሰመየ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ቡላ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይንግርዎ ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ወወረደ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ኅብስተ ፤ ዮርባን ፡ ወጽዋዓ ፡ ወይን ፡ ወቀዲሶ ፡ መጠዎሙ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ። ወሶበ ፡ኮ ኖ ፡ ለሕፃን ፡ 8ዓመተ ፡ ነበበ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ 8፳አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፳ወልድ፣ ቅዱስ ፤ ፳ውእቱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አዕረፉ ፡ አቡ ሁ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ ወለኅዳር‹" ።
ordered him to go to the house of 'Abrehäm and to baptize the child. Having arrived (at the house), he made him enter Christianity (and) called him Bulä. Upon hearing (this), his father and mother were astonished that he had called him by the name of Bulä without them having told him. When the patriarch prayed, the Eucharistic bread and the chalice of wine descended from heaven and, having consecrated (them), he administered the holy mysteries to them. When the child was one year old, he spoke perfectly, saying: "One is the holy Father, one the holy Son, one the holy Spirit." A few days later, his father and mother died on the seventeenth of Hedär.
ወሶበ ፡ ኮኖ ፡ ለኅፃን ፣ ፲ዓመተ ፡ በጽሐ ፤ መኩንን ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ዘይኤዝዝ ፡ ሰጊደ ፡ ለጣዖት ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አባ ፡ ቡላ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወረገመ ፡ አማልክቲሁ ፡ርኩሳ ነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ናዕሰ ፡ አካሉ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይቀንው ዎ ፡ በቅንዋት ፡ ወይስትሩ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወይስሐብዎ፤፤ ፡ ወይዝሐቁ ፡ አነዳሁ፤፤ ፡ ወይግዝፅዎ፤ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ ፡ ወይቅሥፉ ፡ ዘባኖ ፡ ወይደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ መንኩራኩር ፡ ማእከለ ፡ አስይፍት ፡ ወኩያንው ፡ ወይስሐብዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ሀገር። ወመጽአ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወአድኃኖ ፡ ወፈወሶ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ካልእ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ወጸረፈ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወተምዕዓ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወመተረ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወተከለለ ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወቿለሚያዝየ ። ወወረደ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወአንሥኦ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወአልበሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ አስኬማ ፡ በአርአያ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አዘዘከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ትኩን ፣ሱታፌሆሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ።
When the child was ten years old, an impious governor arrived who ordered the worship of idols. Learning (this), Abba Bulä went to the governor and cursed his impure gods. When the governor saw the smallness of his stature, he was greatly astonished; immediately, he ordered him to be nailed with nails, his body to be torn, to be stretched, his skin to be flayed, his hands and feet to be sawn off, his back to be whipped, to be put on a wheel in the midst of swords and spears, and to be dragged on the road of the city, (and it was done so). The angel Mikä'el (= Michael) came, saved him, and healed him. Afterward, (Bulä) went to another impious governor and insulted him. The governor became angry and had his head cut off with a sword, and (Bulä) was crowned (with the crown of martyrdom) on the eighteenth of Miyäzyä. Mikä'el descended from heaven, resurrected him without harm, led him to the desert, clothed him in the monastic garment, gave him the scapular in the likeness of the sign of the cross, and said to him: "God orders you to be the companion of the saints."
ወዓርገ ፡ አባ ፡ ቡላ ፣ መልዕልተ ፡ ኦም ፡ ይቡስ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትጋደል ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አጽርዖ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይዜከር ፡ ስቅለቶ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወሕማማቲ ሁ ፡ ይትዌረው ፡ እምላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፅፅ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ! ። ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ፤ ፡ ሶበ ፣፡ ተወርወ ፡ ጸንዓ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ለቀቲሎቱ! ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወአንሥኦ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢይኩን ፡ ስምከ ፡ ቡላ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ አላ ፡ አቢብሃ ፡ እስመ ፡ አበ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ትከውን ። ወወሰከ ፡ አፍቅሮቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር!” ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸፍዕ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ወይመትር ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ በበግማድ፤ ፡ ወይቀሥፍ፤" ፡ ዘባኖ ፡ ኒ፻ ጊዜ ። ወበዝግብር ፡ ሞ ተ ፡፻ጊዜ፤ቦ ፡ ወአንሥኦ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ ወለለእጉድ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ እምድንግል ፡ ወበካልእ ፡ ዕለታት ፡ ተእኅዞቶ ፡ ወሕማማቲሁ ፡እምነ ፡ አይሁድ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፤ ኢይበልዕ ፣ ወኢይሰቲ ፣ ሣወ፪ዓመተ ፡ ወኢያሰምክ ፡ በገቦሁ ፤ ፳ወ2"" ። ወካዕበ ፡ ተተክለ ፡ በርእሱ ፡ ፲ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ ወፄአውራኃ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኃልቀ ፡ ናላሁ ። ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ነጸረ ፡ ሕማሞ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ተከለ ፡ ሰይፈ ፡ መንጸሮ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወሞተ ። ወመጽአ ት ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ምስለ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በድኑ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ፍቁርየ ። ወነበበ ፡ በድኑ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለኪ፥ ፡ ኦንግሥተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም። ወረሰየት ፡ እደዊሃ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወፈወሰቶ ።
Abba Bulä climbed onto a withered bush and remained there in (spiritual) combat without respite. When he remembered the crucifixion of our Lord and his sufferings, he would throw himself down from the bush all the time. One day, as he had thrown himself down, Satan was (strong) enough to kill him, but our Savior came then, resurrected him, and said to him: "May your name not only be Bulä, but (also) 'Abib, for you will be the father of many." (Bulä) redoubled his love for God and he would slap his face, cut his body piece by piece, and whip his back seven hundred times. In this way, he died three times and our Savior resurrected him. Every Sunday, our Savior showed him how he was born of the Virgin. On other days, (he showed him) his capture and his sufferings (inflicted) by the Jews. Because of this, (Bulä) remained without eating or drinking for forty-two years and he did not rest on his side for sixty-eight (years). He also fixed himself by the head for twelve years and seven months until his skull broke. One day, as he was contemplating the Passion of our Lord, he planted a sword in front of him, threw himself onto it, and died. Our Lady Mary came with the angels to where his corpse was and said to him: "Peace to you, my beloved." His corpse spoke and said to him: "Peace to you, queen of the whole universe." (The Virgin) put her hands on his body and healed it.

When his days were fulfilled so that he had to leave this world, our Lord Jesus Christ appeared to him, with crowns of gold and various precious stones and resplendent garments in his hand. He said to him: "Come to me, my beloved 'Abib, so that I may rejoice you with the joy that does not perish. I have sworn by myself that, to anyone who invokes your name, I will forgive sins, and (that) I will forgive the sins up to the tenth generation (of) anyone who will make your commemoration or will have pity on the poor or who will clothe the one who is naked or who will satiate the hungry or who will give drink to the thirsty or who will build a chapel or who will have written your life or who will read it or who will listen to it." Having said this, he kissed his mouth, carried him on his chest, and made him ascend into the air. When his soul heard the song of glory of the angels, it separated from his body and entered the heavenly Jerusalem. May his prayer and his blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]
"ወተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ”" ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ" ፡ አምሳለ ፡ መላእክት ። ወለቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ፤ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ አሞኒ ፡ 'እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አክሚም ፡ ወስማ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ኢስያ፥፤ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ጸድቃነ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወየሐው ሩ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖቱ ፡ ወያፈቅርዎሙ ፡ ለፈላሲያን ። ወኢኮነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ወርእየት፥ ፡ እሙ፥ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ራእየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ብርሃናዊ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ፅፅ ፡ ወተከላ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ወሠረፀት ፡ ወጸገየት ፡ ወፈረየት ። ወይቤላ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብርሃናዊ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይበልዕ ፡ እምዛቲ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ የሐዩ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወነሥአት ፡ እ ሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እምፍሬሃ ፡ ወበልዓት??-5 ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ጥዑመ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፉሃ ። ወትቤ ፡ በልባ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ ይከውነኒ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ፍሬ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሀት ፡ እምንዋማ ፡ ነገረ ቶ ፥ ለምታ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ርእየት ። ወውእቱ ፡'ካዕበ ፡ አይድዓ፤ ፡ "ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ርእየ፣ በከመ። ፡ ርእየት ፡ ብእሲቱ ። ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወወሰኩ ፡ ገቢ ረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወተጋድሎ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ይጸውሙ ፡ በበ ፪መዓልት ፡ ወሲሳዮሙ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ወፄው ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸንሰት ፡ እሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ትጹሊ ፡ ጸሎታተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወትመውኦሙ ፡ ለፅዕደው ። ወኮነት ፡ ትሰግድቶ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ለለ ፡ ሌሊት፥ ፡ ፲፻ ስግደተ ፡ "ወለለ ፤ መዓልት ፡ "፲፻ ወ ፳፻ ፡ ስግደተ፤ ፡ መጠነ ፡፪8 አውራኅ ፡ እስከ ፡ ወለደቶ ፡ ለሕ[9]ን ። ወሰመይዎ ፡ አብሎ፥። ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወሰኩ ፡
And (there is) the commemoration of the holy Abba 'Ablo (= Apollon), image of the angels. The name of the father of the saint 'Ablo was 'Amoni, from the people of the city of 'Akmim (= Akhmim). The name of his mother (was) 'Isyä. Both were righteous before God, walked in his way, and loved travelers. They had no child. One night, his mother saw a vision: (it was) like a luminous man, (he had) with him a tree and he planted it in her (= the woman's) house. It sprouted, blossomed, and bore fruit. The luminous (man) said to her: "Whoever eats of this fruit will live eternally." The mother of the saint took of its fruit, ate it, and it was sweet in her mouth. She said in her heart: "Will there be a fruit for me?" When she awoke from her sleep, she told her husband how she had seen (a vision). He, in his turn, told his wife that he had seen (a vision) like she had seen. They praised God greatly and redoubled their righteous actions and (spiritual) combat; they both fasted every two days and their food (was) bread and salt. When the mother of the saint Abba 'Ablo conceived, she made many prayers and surpassed men (in her austerities). She made a thousand prostrations every night and a thousand five hundred prostrations every day for nine months until she gave birth to the child. They named him 'Ablo, then redoubled their beautiful actions and righteous actions.
ገቢረ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ 'ወገቢረ ፤ ጽድቅ ። ወሶበ ፡ኮነ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ፈተወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይልበሰስ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወነበረ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይረክብ ፡ ምክንያተ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዓርከ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አቢብ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወወፅኡ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ወመንኩሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፳ደብር ፡ እም ነ ፡ አድባራተ ፡ "ላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ" ። ወኮኑ ፡ ይፀመዱ ፡ ወይትጋደሉ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ።
When Abba 'Ablo was an adolescent, he desired to put on the monastic garment. He remained thus until he had found an opportunity. He had a friend whose name (was) 'Abib. He took him with him, they went out at night, and they went and became monks in a monastery among the monasteries of Upper Egypt. They

ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ አቢብ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ወ፳ለወርኃ ፡ ጥቅምት ። አሜሃ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አብሉግ" ፡ ወተጋብ ኡ ፤ ኀቤሁ፤ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ“ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር“ ፡ ወአምልኮ ፡ ሠናይ ። ወበዕለተ ፡ ፅረፍቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አቢብ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገብሩ" ፡ ተዝካሮ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡አ ባ ፡ አብሎ" ፡ ኦአኅው ፡ ዘይጹሊ ፡ "ዮም ፡ ጸሎተ” ፡ በስሙ ፡ "ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ""፣ አቢብ ፡ ይሰሪ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ "እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ?፤-?።" ፡ ኃጢአቶ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተካየዶ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ በዝንቱ ፡ ኪዳን ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ኅሩይየ" ፡ አቢብ ፡፣ ዘይጹሊ ፡ "አሐተ ፡ ጸሎተ”፤ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ተዝካርከ” ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኦአምላኩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አቢብ ፡ ስረይ ፡ሊተ፣ ኃጢአትየ ፡ አነ ፤ አኃድግ ፡ ሎቱ?" ፡ ኃጢአቶ?-” ። ወኮነ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ አዕረ ፈ ፤ ፳እምነ ፡ አኃው ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ወይገንዝዎ ። ናፈቀ" ፡ አሐዱ፣ እምነ፤ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ምውት ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትናፍቁ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ለአቡ ነ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ ። እስመ ፡ 'እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተካየዶ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ኪዳን ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ አባ ፡ አቢብ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ተዝካሩ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ውእቱጾ ፡ ምውትጾ ፡ ዘን ተ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ወሰከበ ። ወአንከሩ ፡ አኃው ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብ
devoted themselves to asceticism and practiced a great (spiritual) combat. Some time later, Abba 'Abib died on the twenty-fifth of the month of Teqemt. Then the holy Abba 'Ablo went to the mountain of 'Ablug (= Abloudj) and many people gathered to him. He taught them to fear God and to serve him well. On the day of the death of Abba 'Abib, while they were making his commemoration, Abba 'Ablo said to them: "My brothers, whoever makes a prayer today in the name of the saint 'Abib, our Lord Christ will forgive him his sins according to what Christ promised (him) by this pact. He said to him: My chosen one 'Abib, the one who will make a prayer on the day of your commemoration and will say: O God of the saint 'Abib, forgive me my sins, I will remit his sins to him." At that moment, one of the brothers died and they (= the other monks) arose and buried him. One of the monks doubted what Abba 'Ablo was saying. Immediately, the dead man arose, began to speak, and said to them: "Why do you doubt the word of our father Abba 'Ablo? Indeed, God made this pact for our father Abba 'Abib on the day of his commemoration." When the dead man had said this to them, he turned around and lay down. The brother monks marveled and praised God.
ሔር ። ወሐይወ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አድባራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፤ ወውሉድዶ ፡ ብዙኃነ ። ወኮነ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለአባ፤ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰም ዓ ፡ በእንቲአሁ። ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወጸሐፈ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልእክ ተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይናዝዞ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወለውሉዱ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወያጸንዖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ሥምረቱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወእንዘ፤ ፡ አባ ፣ መቃርስ ፡ ይጽሕፍ ፡ ይእተ ፣ መልእ[ክ]ተ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ አእመረ ፡ አባ ፡ አብሎ” ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እን ዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ እለ ፡ የዐውድዎ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይትናገሩ" ፡ በቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አርምሙ ፡ ኦአኃው ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ” ፡ አቡነ” ፣ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ!0 ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ለነ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ምልአት ፡ ኑዛዜ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተናገረ ፤ በዝንቱ፤፤ ፡ በሕዳጥ፤ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ፳መነኮስ ፣ ወምስሌሁ ፡ መልእክቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ መቃርስ፤ ። ወፅኡ ፡ መነኮሳት ፤ ወተቀበልዎ ፡ በፍሥሓ፤ ፡
Abba 'Ablo lived for many years; he had many monasteries and many (spiritual) sons. He lived in the time of Abba Magäres (= Macarius) the Great. When Abba Magäres heard (about) him, he rejoiced in him and wrote him a letter comforting him and his (spiritual) sons, the monks, and strengthening them in the execution of the will of God. While Abba Magäres was writing this letter in the desert of 'Asqêtes (= Scetis), Abba 'Ablo knew (it) by the Holy Spirit while he was in Upper Egypt. He said to the monks who surrounded him while conversing about the word of God: "Be silent, my brothers, for behold, our father Abba Magäres the Great has written us a letter which (is) full of comfort." After he had said this, a little (time) later, a monk arrived, (he had) with him the letter of Abba Magäres. The monks went out, welcomed him with joy, and it (= the letter) was read before all the monks, and they experienced great joy.
ወአንበብዋ! ፡ በቅድመ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ መነኮሳት ፤ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፍሥሓ ። ወዝንቱ፤‹የ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ፤ ፡ አብሎ፤" ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ፣ አሞንዮስ ፡ ወርእያ ፡ ለ[ይ]እቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ የዋሂት ፡ ትነብር ፡ በኀቤሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ "ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርፎ፤' ፡ እምጻማ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለ ም፤!ቦ ፡ አፅረፈ ፡ በሰላም፤!፤ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ለወርኃ ፡ የካቲት ። ወንሕነሰ ፡ ጸሐፍነ ፡
It is this holy Abba 'Ablo who went to the holy Abba 'Amonyos (= Ammonius) and who saw this holy woman whose name (was) Yawahit (= the sweet) dwelling near him. When Christ wanted to make him rest from the fatigue of this world, he died in peace on the fifth of the month of

ዜናሁ፤፤። ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዓርኩ ፡ አባ ፡ አቢብ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜ ን። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወብፁዕ ፡ መስተጋድል!፤-፤፤ ፣ አባ ፡ አቢብ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መንኩሰ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወአጻመወ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወሰጊድ ፡ ወትጋህ ፡፤ ወተፀምዶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወብዝኃ ፡ ጸማሁ ። አስተርአዮ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወናዘዞ ፡ ወአጽንዖ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ የአክለከ ፡ ኦኅሩይየ ፡ አቢ ብ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ጸማ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አስተዳለውኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ ዕረፍት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። አማን ፡ እብለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይጹሊ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ተዝካርከ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኦአምላኩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አቢብ ፡ ኅድግ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አበሳየ ፡ አነ ፡ አሰሪ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኃጢአቶ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስምከ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘን ተ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፍሥሓ ። ወዓርገ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሐመ ፡ንስቲተ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አቢብ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወወረሰ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ዘለዓለም ።
Yakkätit. As for us, we have written his history with (that of) his friend Abba 'Abib. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. On this day also died the holy and blessed father, the athlete Abba 'Abib. This saint became a monk from his youth. He waged a great (spiritual) combat, tired himself for many years in fasting and in prayer, adoration, vigil, devotion to God, and the greatness of his labor. Our Lord Christ - glory to him! - appeared to him, comforted him, strengthened him, and said to him: "My chosen one 'Abib, this labor suffices for you, for behold, I have prepared for you a dwelling in eternal rest. In truth, I tell you that whoever will make a prayer on the day of your commemoration and will say: 'God of the saint Abba 'Abib, remit my faults', I, I will forgive him his sins for your name." When our Lord Christ had said this to him, ('Abib) experienced great joy and our Lord ascended to heaven. After that, the holy Abba 'Abib suffered a little, died in peace, and inherited eternal life. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። "ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ፤፤ ፡ ቅዳሴሃ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘቅዱስ፤! ፡ ዩልዮስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አቅፋስ፤፤" ። እስመ፤፤ ፡ ቅዱስ፤፤ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ዘሀገ ረ! ፡ ጥው!፤፤ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ በገድሉ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ጥፍአቱ ፡ ለዲዮቅልጢያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ! ፡ ወነግሠ፤፤ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ጸድቅ፤!፤ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይጠመቅ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ በሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ። ወሶበ ፡ ተጠምቀ ፡ ተለዓለ፡፡፤ ፡ ቀርነ ፡ "መንግሥተ ፡ ክርስቲያን፤ ፡ ወተሐንጹ፤" ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡፤ በስሞሙ ፡ ለሰማዕታት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ እለ ፤ ቀተልዎሙ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ከሓዲያን ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ 'ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ፤፤ ፡ ዩልዮስ፤። ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ አቀሞ፤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ወዓቀቦ፤፤ ፡ ከመ ፣ የሐሊ ፡ በሥጋሆሙ ፡ ለሰማዕታ ት፤›። ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወአግብርቲሁ ። ወይጸውር፤ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ወይገንዞሙ፤ ፡ ወይጽሕፍ፤፤ ፡ ገድሎሙ ። "ወእፎ ፡ እምድኅረዝ፤ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሰማዕተ ። ወደሶ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ፤፤" ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ፤፤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ "ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ፤ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕንጹ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፤ ሠናይተ ፡ ወይጹሩ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወያንብርዎ ፡ ውስቴታ ። ወሐነ ጹ፤ ፥ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ሠናይተ፤፤ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአፍለሱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዩልዮስ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፣፤ ውስቴታ ። ወቀደሳ ፡ "ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት!፤ ፡ አባ፤፣ እልስክንድሮስ፤ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆልሳተ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ላቲ ፡ በዐለ ፡ ዓቢ
On this day also (there was) the consecration of the church of the saint Yulyos (= Julius), martyr of the city of 'Agfäs (= Agfahs). When the saint became a martyr of the city of Tew (= Taouah), as (it is) written in his life - this (took place) thus: after the destruction of Diyogletiyänos (= Diocletian) the impious, Quastantinos (= Constantine) the Just reigned, (this was) a short time before he was baptized with Christian baptism - and when (he) was baptized, the power of the Christian empire was raised and churches were built in the name of the holy martyrs whom the impious emperors had killed. When the emperor Quastantinos learned the history of this saint Yulyos and how God had raised him and preserved him so that he would take care of the bodies of the martyrs, he and his servants - he carried their bodies, buried them, and wrote their life -, and how after that he became a martyr, Quastantinos (therefore) praised this saint, for his life was beautiful. The emperor sent much money to the land of Egypt and ordered that a beautiful church be built for him, (that) his body be carried and (that) it be placed there. A beautiful church was built, according to what the emperor had ordered, the body of the saint Yulyos was transferred and placed there. The patriarch Abba 'Eleskenderos (= Alexander) consecrated it with his bishops and made a great feast for it like this day. There were from the body of the saint Yulyos


[p. 148] {#po.44.p148}
የ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወኮነ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዩልዮስ፤ ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፡ ዓበይተ፤" ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፤ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አቤል ፡ ወደሊላ ፡ ድንግል ። በረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
great miracles and wonders. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. [And on this day (is) the commemoration of Abba 'Abel and (of) Dalilà the virgin. May their blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu for ever and ever, amen.]
አመ ፡ ፳5ሄለጥቅምት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ፤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'ሐዋርያ ፡ ጢሞና፦- ፡ ሰማዕት ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ኀረዮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ እምኖልቆሙ ፡ ለድወ፪አርድእት ፡ ዘኃረዮሙ ፤ ወአርአዮሙ ፥ ወፈነዎሙቶ ፡ ወወሀቦሙ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈውሱ ፡ ዱያነ ፡ ወያውፅኡ ፡ ሰይጣናተ ። ወነሥአ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ሰማያዊተ ፡ ወጸጋ ፡ መለኮታዊተ” ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ፈወሶሙ ፡ ለድውያን" ፡ ወገረሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሰይጣናት ። ወተለአኮ፤ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶ ስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ይነብር ፡ በሥጋ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ። ወእምድኅረ፤፤ ፡ ዓርገ ፤ እግዚእነ፤። ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይትለአኮሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያ ት፤! ፡ ወትረ፤ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ!፤ ፡ ወላዕሌሁ! ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ዛ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ኃረይዎ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ እምጥፕልቆሙ ፡ ለዲያቆናት ፡ እለ ፡ ጫምዎሙ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ 'መጽሐፍ ፡ ስምዓ!" ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ምሉኣነ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወጥበብ! ። ወእምድኅ ረ ፤ ተለአከ፤ ፡ በመልእክተ ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ ሕዳጠ ፤ መዋዕለ" ፡ ሜምዎ ፡ ቀሲሰ ። ወእምድኅረዝ፤፤ ፡ አንበሩ ፡ እደ ፡ ላዕሌሁ" ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆይፅስ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ብስራ ፡ ዘምፅራብ ፡ ዘእምብሔረ"" ፡ በልቃ- ። ወሰበከ ፣ ውስቴታ ፡ በሃይማኖተ ፡፤ እግዚእነ፡ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአጥመቆሙ-?" ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ እምነ ፡ ዮናናዊያን ፡ ወአይሁድ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ አኃዞ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጢሞና ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ "ዓቢየ ፡ ኩነኔ፤ጾ ፡ ወአውዓዮ ፡ በእሳት ። ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡
[READING] FOR 26 TEQEMT. In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day the holy apostle Timonä (= Timon) became a martyr. Our Lord chose this disciple among the number of the seventy-two disciples whom he chose, (whom) he made see, (whom) he sent, and to whom he gave the power to heal the sick and to cast out demons (from the possessed). This saint received the heavenly gift and divine grace and, through them, he healed the sick, and the demons submitted to him. He served our Lord Christ - glory to him! - while he dwelt in the flesh on the earth. After our Lord had ascended to heaven, this saint constantly served the apostles until the grace of the Holy Spirit, the Paraclete, descended upon them and upon him on the day of the feast of Pentecost. After that, the apostles chose him among the number of the seven deacons whom they instituted, concerning whom the Scripture is witness that they were filled with the Holy Spirit and wisdom. After he had served for some time in the office of deacon, they ordained him priest. After that, they laid hands on him (as) bishop in the city of Besrä (= Bostra) of the West which (is part) of the land of Balgä. He preached there the faith of our Lord Christ and baptized many of the Greeks and Jews. When the governor of this city learned this, he (had) the saint Timonä seized, (had) him undergo great torture, and (had) him thrown into the fire. He received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ሰማያት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ እሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወሁራስ፤ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day (is) the commemoration of the saint Yä'qob (= James), the brother of our Lord, and (of) Huräs the martyr. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
”ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ዕረፍቶሙ ፡ ለፊልልፅስ ፡ ወሳውግና ። በረከተ ፡ ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወአስ[ተ]ብቀፆቶሙ ፡ ወትንብልና ፤ ሣህሎሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡፤ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also (is) the death of Filpos (= Philip) and (of) Sawgnä. May the blessing of their prayer and their supplication and the intercession of their sweetness be with our king 'Iyäsu for ever and ever, amen.]
